Method: 以语言提供技能选择的显式结构，并将语言指定的任务与其视觉场景中的操作目标关联；框架结合分层任务选择与任务上下文感知模仿学习，把语言指令落地到空间视觉表征上，支持无显式物体标注的拆解操作。

Result: 在多样连接件几何与装配构型上实现泛化，且无需显式物体标注；端到端任务成功率相较扩散策略基线提升35个百分点，相较先前的任务上下文感知基线提升75个百分点。

Conclusion: 语言任务上下文可有效缓解长时序拆解中技能选择歧义与组合泛化难题，为语言引导的机器人拆解提供了可泛化的分层模仿学习范式。

Abstract: Real-world robotic disassembly requires long-horizon execution, where robots must perform ordered sequences of manipulation tasks across multiple parts within a single scene. Multiple valid task goals and diverse assembly configurations make it difficult for imitation policies to infer the intended skill from raw observations alone, particularly when training data cannot cover the combinatorial diversity of real-world configurations and part geometries. We show that incorporating task context through language alleviates these challenges by providing explicit structure for skill selection and associating language-specified tasks with their corresponding manipulation targets in the visual scene. The proposed framework combines hierarchical task selection with task-context-aware imitation learning to ground language instructions in spatial visual representations for robotic disassembly. The resulting framework generalizes across diverse connector geometries and assembly configurations without requiring explicit object annotations. Our method improves end-to-end task success by 35 percentage points over the baseline diffusion policy and by 75 percentage points over the previous task-context-aware baseline.

</details>


### [105] [RAF-VLA: Representation Alignment with the Future for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17728)
*Dogun Kim,Yongjae Lee,Joonhee Lim,Yeina Lee,Junhyeok Park,Moogeun Park,Dongsuk Kum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAF-VLA 提出用"未来帧表征对齐"替代"显式未来生成"来塑造 VLA 策略的内部表征，仅在监督微调时加一项正则项，即可获得世界模型式监督的规划收益，同时避免生成式世界模型带来的训练负担与推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶 VLA 结合世界模型时依赖显式生成未来驾驶场景作为密集监督，虽能促使策略学到有利于规划的丰富内部表征，但带来两个关键缺陷：额外的训练负担和推理延迟。作者希望保留"未来信息作为表征监督"的收益，同时去掉生成过程本身的开销。

Method: 提出 RAF-VLA（Representation Alignment with the Future）框架，核心是 Future-Aligned Supervised Fine-Tuning：在学习驾驶动作的同时，用一项简单的正则化项将策略的隐藏状态与预训练世界编码器提取的未来帧表征对齐。该方法不再要求模型显式生成未来帧，而是以未来表征为直接引导信号塑造规划相关的内部表征。

Result: 在 NAVSIM 基准上的大量实验表明，RAF-VLA 以显著更少的训练样本量达到与当前最优 VLA 规划器相当的规划性能，同时仅引入约 3.8% 的训练开销和可忽略的约 1 ms 推理开销。

Conclusion: 以未来帧表征对齐替代显式未来生成，是获取世界模型式监督收益的一种低成本、高效率途径；该方法在保持规划竞争力的同时几乎不增加训练与推理成本，为 VLA 自动驾驶中的世界建模提供了轻量化替代范式。

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for autonomous driving have incorporated world modeling by predicting future driving scenes alongside driving actions, demonstrating strong planning performance. Future driving scenes are utilized as dense supervision, encouraging the policy to learn rich internal representations useful for planning. However, these World-Modeling VLAs rely on explicit future generation to learn such representations, thereby introducing two key limitations: additional training burden and inference latency. To address these limitations, we propose RAF-VLA (Representation Alignment with the Future), a VLA-based autonomous driving framework that shapes planning-relevant internal representations through direct guidance from future-frame representations. RAF-VLA employs Future-Aligned Supervised Fine-Tuning, in which a straightforward regularization aligns the policy's hidden states with future-frame representations obtained from a pretrained world encoder while learning driving actions. This simple alignment allows RAF-VLA to avoid the training burden and inference latency associated with future generation. Extensive experiments on the NAVSIM benchmark show that RAF-VLA achieves competitive planning performance against state-of-the-art VLA planners with substantially fewer training samples seen. Moreover, RAF-VLA incurs only 3.8% training overhead and a negligible 1 ms inference overhead.

</details>


### [106] [CALOS: Control-Affine Lyapunov On-manifold Safety Layer for Safe Deep Reinforcement Learning for Quadrotors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17758)
*Fabrizio Cesareo,Sebastiano Mengozzi,Nicola Mimmo,Andrea Acquaviva*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CALOS：面向四旋翼深度强化学习的控制仿射Lyapunov在流形安全层，将四个倾角不等式与Lyapunov下降条件建模为单个QP，对策略标称力矩求最小范数修正，通过三维力矩空间上的activeset枚举精确求解，可在Isaac Lab中实时约束数千并行环境，无约束违反且横向跟踪误差降低55-60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习在四旋翼控制上表现出色，但学到的策略在训练与部署阶段均无安全约束保证，姿态（倾角）约束尤其难以保证。作者希望在不修改底层学习算法的前提下，提供一种运行时可插拔的安全层，使策略在探索与执行过程中始终满足姿态约束。

Method: CALOS（Control-Affine Lyapunov On-manifold Safety）将四个倾角不等式约束与一个Lyapunov下降条件统一写成一个二次规划问题，目标为对策略输出的标称力矩做最小范数修正。由于力矩空间仅为三维，QP通过对activeset的枚举精确求解，计算开销低，可支撑现代大规模并行DRL训练中数千个并行仿真环境的实时约束执行。

Result: 在NVIDIA Isaac Lab的轨迹跟踪任务上，相比未加约束的PPO基线，CALOS使横向跟踪误差降低55-60%，并在训练轨迹上实现零姿态约束违反；将探索限制在安全状态空间区域后，还加快了训练收敛、提升了数据效率，且未产生次优策略。

Conclusion: 结果说明将控制理论（控制仿射Lyapunov/流形安全）与运行时QP安全滤波结合，可作为DRL四旋翼控制的通用、算法无关安全层：既严格保证姿态约束零违反，又改善跟踪性能与训练效率，适合大规模并行仿真训练。局限在于验证集中于仿真与单一任务（轨迹跟踪、姿态约束），真实硬件部署与更一般约束形式尚待验证。

Abstract: Deep Reinforcement Learning has demonstrated remarkable capability in quadrotor control, yet learned policies offer no guarantee of respecting safety constraints during training or deployment. We present CALOS (Control-Affine Lyapunov On-manifold Safety), a runtime safety layer that enforces attitude constraints on a quadrotor without modifying the underlying learning algorithm. CALOS formulates four tilt-angle inequalities and a Lyapunov descent condition as a single quadratic program whose solution is the minimum-norm correction to the nominal torque output of the policy. The quadratic program is solved exactly via active-set enumeration over the three-dimensional torque space, with a computational cost low enough to enforce constraints in real time across thousands of parallel simulation environments, as required by modern massively parallel Deep Reinforcement Learning training. Evaluated on trajectory-tracking tasks in NVIDIA Isaac Lab, CALOS reduces lateral tracking error by 55-60% relative to an unconstrained Proximal Policy Optimization baseline while achieving zero attitude-constraint violations on the training trajectory. By restricting exploration to safe regions of the state space, the safety layer also accelerates training convergence and improves data efficiency without producing suboptimal policies.

</details>


### [107] [DRT&R: Direct Radar Teach & Repeat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17766)
*Alexander Krawciw,Daniil Lisus,Cedric Le Gentil,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DRT&R：将直接法旋转雷达处理与局部建图结合的导航栈，在公路与越野环境实现厘米级定位与闭环自主跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雷达对雨雾烟尘等恶劣条件鲁棒，优于易失效的激光雷达方案；已有直接法（保留整帧雷达扫描而非稀疏点）提升了公路全局定位性能，但尚未用于越野环境或闭环系统。此外，直接全局地图会平滑掉视线相关的雷达伪影，丢失在相似轨迹下可提供位姿信息的关键细节。

Method: 提出 Direct Radar Teach &amp; Repeat (DRT&amp;R)，将直接雷达处理与局部建图相结合以最大化保留信息量，构建基于旋转雷达的直接法导航栈（含 MPC 闭环控制）。

Result: 使用 344 km 公路与 20 km 越野数据，在多数公路/越野条件下定位误差小于 4 cm，在几何退化与稀疏环境中为 12 cm；在 Clearpath Warthog 越野车上以 MPC 闭环自主运行超 10 km，实时运行并达到越野雷达导航 SOTA 跟踪性能。

Conclusion: 直接法雷达 + 局部建图可同时提升公路与越野定位精度，并支撑实时闭环自主导航，验证了该方案在恶劣条件下的实用性与 SOTA 性能。

Abstract: Radar-based navigation is appealing for its robustness to adverse conditions involving airborne particles, such as precipitation, dust, fog, and smoke, that can cause lidar-based systems to fail. Recently, direct methods that retain and use the entire radar scan rather than sparse points have improved on-road global localization performance. However, they have yet to be deployed in off-road environments or in closed-loop systems. Additionally, even direct global maps may lose information: their global nature leads to a smoothing out of viewpoint-dependent radar artifacts, which can provide pose information when mapping and localization occur along similar trajectories. This paper introduces Direct Radar Teach & Repeat (DRT&R): a direct spinning radar-based navigation stack that maximizes the amount of retained information by combining direct radar processing with local mapping. DRT&R yields state-of-the-art (SOTA) localization performance in both on-road and off-road environments. Using 344 km of on-road data and 20 km of off-road data, DRT&R is able to localize to within 4 cm in most on-road and off-road conditions, and 12 cm in geometrically degenerate and sparse environments. DRT&R is also evaluated autonomously in closed loop with an MPC controller for more than 10 km using a Clearpath Warthog off-road vehicle, demonstrating that it runs in real time and achieves SOTA tracking performance for off-road radar navigation.

</details>


### [108] [HINT-Plan: Human Intention-Aware Robot Task Planning in Context-Rich Environments using Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17771)
*Yuchen Liu,Luigi Palmieri,Lujun Li,Radu State,Ilche Georgievski,Marco Aiello*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HINT-Plan 将视觉语言模型（VLM）推断的高层人类意图转化为目标状态，结合层次化场景图，融入形式化多智能体任务规划，实现人感知的机器人主动决策，联合任务规划成功率 69.71%，较基线提升最高 35.29%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将人类意识融入移动机器人决策的工作多聚焦于低层运动规划中的碰撞避免，忽视了人类存在及高层行为带来的挑战，缺少在任务规划层面显式考虑人类意图的方法。

Method: 采用 VLM 从第三人称图像观测预测高层人类意图，将其转换为目标状态；使用层次化场景图（SG）表征环境，将环境拓扑与可操作知识翻译为形式化规划语言，求解人-机器人联合任务规划问题，生成可执行计划。

Result: 在逼真仿真环境中，HINT-Plan 的联合人-机器人任务规划总体成功率达 69.71%，较基线最高提升 35.29%，并减少了功能冲突。

Conclusion: 显式地将推断出的人类意图纳入形式化多智能体任务规划，可有效实现主动的人感知机器人决策。

Abstract: Approaches to incorporating human awareness into mobile robot decision-making mainly focus on collision avoidance in low-level motion planning, often overlooking the challenges posed by human presence and high-level behavior. To address this vacancy, we present HINT-Plan, a novel approach to integrate human intention prediction into robot task planning. HINT-Plan employs Vision Language Models (VLMs) to anticipate high-level human intentions from third-person image observations, convert them into goal states, and solve joint task-planning problems. To effectively enable scene awareness in context-rich environments, we use hierarchical Scene Graphs (SGs) as high-level representations of the environment, and translate environmental topology and actionable knowledge into formal planning language to ensure executable plans. Evaluated in a photorealistic simulation, HINT-Plan achieves an overall success rate of 69.71% in joint human-robot task planning, substantially outperforming the baselines by up to 35.29%, while also reducing functional conflicts. The results show the effectiveness of explicitly incorporating inferred human intentions into formal multi-agent task planning for proactive human-aware robot decision-making.

</details>


### [109] [SPROUT: The Open-Source Soft Growing Robot for Search and Rescue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17781)
*Antonio Alvarez Valdivia,Ciera McFarland,Robert Reeve,Ankush Dhawan,Chad Council,Megan Richardson,Margaret McGuinness,Nathaniel Hanson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出模块化软体机器人系统 SPROUT，面向城市搜救场景，经多年与专业搜救队伍协作迭代，具备硬件模块化与 ROS 2 软件栈，并公开代码、CAD 与装配说明以支持复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人长期被视为搜救理想方案，但难以从实验室走向现场，缺乏可复制、可靠的系统与开放构建资料，形成工程落地缺口。

Method: 设计并工程化 SPROUT（Soft Pathfinding Robotic Observation Unit）：模块化硬件支持任务专用载荷与控制功能，ROS 2 软件栈支撑可扩展控制；通过多年与专业城市搜救社区互动确定在粉尘、潮湿、孤立环境下的运行目标；公开代码、零件清单、CAD 装配与搭建说明。

Result: 在急救人员使用的倒塌结构训练场地中，完成多种具有挑战性的巡查与穿越任务，验证系统在现场类条件下的可用性；系统亦可扩展至基础设施巡检、考古等应用。

Conclusion: SPROUT 提供了可复现、可扩展的软体搜救机器人平台与开放构建生态，为软体机器人从实验室向现场部署提供了工程范式，但方法创新偏向系统集成，未见核心算法突破。

Abstract: Soft robotic systems have long been theorized as ideal candidates for use in search and rescue operations; however, there have been significant barriers to entry in graduating soft robotic systems from the laboratory to the field. To address this gap in replicable, reliable soft robot systems, we present the designs for SPROUT, the Soft Pathfinding Robotic Observation Unit. The system has matured over years of interaction with professional urban search and rescue communities, with the goal of operating in dusty, wet, and isolated conditions. This manuscript contains supplementary material, including code, parts manifests, CAD assemblies, and build instructions to allow the broader robotics research community to build their own SPROUT systems. The modular hardware and ROS 2-based software stack support task-specific payloads and control functions, allowing SPROUT to be adapted for applications beyond search and rescue, including infrastructure inspection and archaeology. Here, we demonstrate SPROUT performing a variety of challenging inspection and traversal tasks in collapsed structure training sites used by first responders. The main project page is available online at this https URL .

</details>


### [110] [Learning Multi-Humanoid Pickup and Transport via Decentralized Object-Centric Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17824)
*Bikram Pandit,Mohitvishnu S. Gadde,Aayam Kumar Shrestha,Alan Fern*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种去中心化、以物体为中心的协同多机人形搬运控制框架：将共享物体划分为局部附着区域，各人形通过无夹爪双臂捏合学习拾取与搬运，统一覆盖单机拾取、多机协同运输与机器人间交接，并在仿真与真机上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多物体（尺寸、重量、几何各异）的人形协同拾取与运输通常需要针对不同任务重新设计控制器与团队规模，缺乏统一的任务无关控制抽象；同时希望降低多机协同训练成本，复用单机技能。

Method: 去中心化控制 + 物体中心表示：为每个机器人分配共享物体上的局部附着区域（attachment region），以该区域作为统一控制接口；无夹爪双臂捏合实现抓持；单体策略训练后可直接迁移到多机协同与机器人间交接，并进行显式多机协同训练以学习共享物体耦合动力学。

Result: 仿真中跨不同团队规模与物体几何均有效；仅用单机拾取数据训练的策略可非平凡地迁移到协同场景，显式多机训练进一步提升性能；实现 sim-to-real 迁移，真机人形完成协同操作任务。

Conclusion: 以附着区域为通用抽象可实现任务无关的去中心化协同人形操作，是兼具泛化（规模、几何）与真实部署的人形协同操作工作；但缺少定量指标、基线对比与规模上限分析。

Abstract: We study cooperative multi-humanoid pickup and transport of objects with varying size, weight, and geometry, requiring robot teams of different sizes. Our approach uses decentralized object-centric control, where each humanoid is assigned a local attachment region on the shared object and learns to realize pickup and transport through gripperless bimanual pinching. This attachment-based interface provides a common control abstraction spanning single-robot pickup, cooperative multi-robot transport, and robot-to-robot handover, without per-task redesign. We find that policies trained only on single-robot pickup already transfer nontrivially to cooperative settings, suggesting that this abstraction captures much of the structure needed for coordination. At the same time, explicit multi-robot training further improves performance, showing that shared-object coupling introduces coordination dynamics that are beneficial to learn directly. We validate the approach in simulation across varying team sizes and object geometries, and demonstrate sim-to-real transfer on hardware, where the learned controllers enable real humanoids to perform cooperative manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [111] [Adaptive-MHE : A Sampling-Based Adaptive MPC for Legged Loco-Manipulation via Moving Horizon Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17832)
*Hossein Keshavarz,Alejandro Ramirez-Serrano,Majid Khadiv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Adaptive-MHE，一种基于移动视界估计（MHE）的在线采样式系统辨识框架，可在运行中估计物体与地形物理参数（质量、摩擦等），并与采样式MPC耦合，实现不确定环境下的自适应 loco-manipulation，性能接近使用真值参数的控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足式机器人在 loco-manipulation 任务中需应对未知的物体与地形参数，仿真与真实参数不匹配导致 sim-to-real gap 降低控制性能；经典 Sys-ID 依赖可微动力学假设，对接触丰富的腿式系统不成立；采样式 Sys-ID 虽避开了该假设，但多为离线执行，无法随环境变化在线自适应。

Method: 以移动视界估计（MHE）为框架构建在线采样式系统辨识：通过大规模并行 rollout 直接匹配仿真与实测状态轨迹，估计物体与地形的物理参数（质量、摩擦等）；并将参数估计结果与采样式模型预测控制（MPC）耦合，形成闭环自适应 loco-manipulation 控制。

Result: 在仿真与硬件实验中，该框架持续优于基线方法，并达到与拥有真值参数的控制器相当的性能，表明在线采样式辨识有效缓解了参数失配带来的性能退化。

Conclusion: 该工作表明将移动视界估计与采样式MPC结合，可实现在线、无需可微动力学假设的参数辨识与自适应 loco-manipulation，是应对未知且时变环境的一条可行路径。

Abstract: Legged robots have demonstrated a remarkable ability to traverse various terrains, yet generating effective loco-manipulation behaviors remains challenging. A key difficulty is that object and terrain parameters are typically unknown to the robot, and mismatches between these parameters and their simulated counterparts introduce a sim-to-real gap that degrades control performance. Classical system identification (Sys-ID) methods often assume differentiable dynamics, an assumption that does not hold for contact-rich legged systems. Sampling-based Sys-ID avoids this restriction by directly matching simulated and recorded state trajectories through massively parallel rollouts, but existing approaches are typically applied offline and do not adapt as environmental conditions change. We present Adaptive-MHE an online sampling-based Sys-ID framework, based on moving horizon estimation (MHE), that estimates the physical parameters of objects and terrain in the environment (e.g., mass, friction) and couples this estimate with a sampling-based model predictive controller, enabling adaptive loco-manipulation in changing and uncertain environments. In simulation and hardware experiments, our framework consistently outperforms baselines and matches the performance of a controller with access to ground-truth parameters.

</details>


### [112] [VCTP: Vehicle-Conditioned Terrain Planning for Off-Road Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17834)
*Akshay Naik,Ramavarapu S. Sreenivas,Dustin Nottage,Ahmet Soylemezoglu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出车辆条件地形规划VCTP，在8个航向下联合评估高程与地表ID层，用车身几何约束可达性、用受载轮接触点计算地表代价与俯仰/侧倾，并结合D* Lite与车辆状态搜索规划前进/倒车路径；接触点采样比车心采样降低建模地表代价39.3%，选择性更新与全量重算在474组对比中一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆航向同时影响轮胎下的地形表面以及车身俯仰和侧倾，但常规地形规划往往在车心或独立采样点上评估地形代价，忽略了航向、车身几何与轮-地接触之间的耦合关系，导致代价估计偏差与路径选择次优。

Method: 共享高程层与地表ID层在8个航向下查询；以车身几何约束可通行性，以受载轮接触点确定建模地表代价及预测俯仰/侧倾；将上述评估嵌入D* Lite与车辆状态搜索，支持前进与倒车运动；观测变化时仅重算受影响的车身或接触查询。

Result: 全观测双轨仿真中，轮接触点采样相较车心采样使建模地表代价降低39.3%并缩短路径；RGator离线规划中建模代价优于距离导向规划，但覆盖不完整导致全程路线排序未能确定；在记录的地图变化下，474组对比中选择性更新与全量重算结果一致。

Conclusion: 结果表明轮-地接触点放置方式与车辆航向会实质影响路径选择，为地形代价建模与车辆条件化规划提供了可复用的评估框架，但真实场景覆盖不足限制了全局排序结论，需进一步验证。

Abstract: A vehicle's heading affects both the surfaces beneath its tires and its pitch and roll. We present Vehicle-Conditioned Terrain Planning (VCTP), which retains these relationships by evaluating shared elevation and surface-ID layers at eight headings. Body geometry constrains admissibility, while loaded wheel contacts determine modeled surface cost and predicted pitch and roll. Established D* Lite and vehicle-state search use these evaluations to plan routes with forward and reverse motion. When observations change, VCTP recomputes every affected body or contact query. In fully observed two-track simulations, sampling at wheel contacts rather than at the vehicle center lowers modeled surface cost by 39.3% while shortening the route. In offline planning on RGator recordings, VCTP also reduces modeled costs over identified surfaces and observed support relative to distance-focused planning, although incomplete coverage leaves full-route rankings unresolved. Selective updates match full recomputation in all 474 comparisons using recorded map changes. These results identify when wheel-contact placement and vehicle heading affect route choice.

</details>


### [113] [Timely Activation of Safety Filters via One-Step Reachability Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17904)
*Javier Borquez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文针对离散时间执行下HJ可达性安全滤波器的失效问题，提出"一步扩张BRT"作为激活条件，保证离散采样下的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hamilton-Jacobi可达性安全滤波器在连续时间下能提供强安全保证，但实际机器人以离散采样间隔施加控制，系统可能在两次更新之间"跳入"不安全的后向可达管（BRT），造成理论上可避免的失效。这一连续-离散不匹配是实际部署中的核心隐患。

Method: 将"单时间步内能到达真实BRT的所有状态"定义为一步扩张BRT，并将其表述为一个修改后的可达性问题；用标准连续时间求解器计算该扩张集，再以其边界作为安全滤波器的触发条件，使干预提前发生。

Result: 摘要未给出具体实验数据；主要主张是：以扩张边界作为激活条件后，安全干预足够提前，从而在离散时间执行下仍保证正确性。摘要中未提供定量或实机验证结果。

Conclusion: 通过重新构造可达性问题并复用标准连续时间求解器，该方法在不改变滤波器最小干预性质的前提下，弥合了连续时间安全理论与离散时间实现之间的差距。其价值在于为离散执行下的安全滤波器提供原则性修正。

Abstract: Least-restrictive safety filters based on Hamilton-Jacobi reachability provide strong safety guarantees by overriding a nominal controller only when the system reaches the boundary of the set of unsafe states defined as a Backward Reachable Tube (BRT). These guarantees, however, rely on the continuous-time nature of the underlying formulation. In practice, robotic systems apply control at discrete sampling intervals, which creates a mismatch where the system may jump into the unsafe BRT between updates, allowing failures that are theoretically avoidable. This work introduces a principled solution based on a one-step expanded BRT that predicts all states capable of reaching the true BRT within a single timestep. By using this expanded boundary as the activation condition for the safety filter, safety interventions occur early enough to ensure correctness under discrete-time execution. We formulate this expanded set as a modified reachability problem and compute it using standard continuous-time solvers.

</details>


### [114] [Map2Route: Benchmarking Compositional Language-Grounded Route Planning over Semantic Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17910)
*Muyi Bao,Hang Xu,Jingfan Tang,Zihan Liu,Yuxin Cai,Chen Lv,Wenshan Wang,Ji Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Map2Route 基准（40 场景、1000 条情节，含关系/比较/嵌套描述与 must-pass、must-avoid、软偏好及作用域约束），并给出 Grounding2Route 方法，结合可执行代码式 grounding、验证引导修复与作用域感知，显著超越 7 个适配基线但距人类示范仍有明显差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言引导路线规划任务对组合式指令（关系/比较/嵌套描述）、有序必经区域、规避要求、多类软偏好及空间与路线阶段作用域的覆盖不全，缺乏系统性评测基准，难以衡量模型在预建语义地图上的组合泛化能力。因此需要一个人工精编的基准与方法基线。

Method: 构建 Map2Route：40 个场景、1000 条情节，基于预建语义地图，指令包含关系、比较与嵌套描述以定位与路线相关的物体和区域，并显式给出有序必经区域、必经规避条件、五类软偏好以及空间与路线阶段作用域。方法 Grounding2Route 采用可执行代码作为 grounding 表示，配合验证引导的修复机制（verification-guided repair）与作用域感知（scope-aware）推理，与七个代表性适配基线对比评测。

Result: 在全部指标上，Grounding2Route 大幅优于现有方法（含七个适配基线）；但与人类示范之间仍存在显著差距，说明任务本身难度高、仍有较大提升空间。论文还提供了定性结果与开源资源。

Conclusion: Map2Route 为组合式语言 grounding 的路线规划提供了较完整的评测设定与明确的难度上限，Grounding2Route 验证了"代码式 grounding + 验证修复 + 作用域感知"的有效性；距离人类水平仍有明显 headroom，是后续研究的重要方向。

Abstract: We introduce Map2Route, a human-curated benchmark for compositional language-grounded route planning over pre-built semantic maps. Map2Route contains 1,000 episodes across 40 scenes, where instructions use relational, comparative, and nested descriptions to identify route-relevant objects and regions, while specifying ordered must-pass regions, must-avoid requirements, five categories of soft preferences, and spatial and route-stage scopes, which is partially tested by existing works. Alongside Map2Route, we propose Grounding2Route, which combines executable code-as-grounding with verification-guided repair and scope-aware this http URL seven representative adapted baselines, Grounding2Route substantially outperforms existing methods in all metrics. Despite these gains, a substantial gap to human demonstrations remains, highlighting the difficulty of Map2Route and the considerable headroom for future progress. Additional qualitative results and resources are available on this https URL .

</details>


### [115] [Multi-Session Multimodal Underwater Mapping with Acoustic and Optical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17929)
*Precious Philip-Ifabiyi,Valerio Franchi,Fausto Ferreira,Nuno Gracias*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于因子图优化的多航次、多模态水下建图框架，联合优化轨迹、三维地标、传感器外参与每航次全局对齐，在加泰罗尼亚海岸真实数据集上提升地图一致性与语义标注精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下测绘对海洋科学、考古与环境监测很重要，但不同传感器（侧扫声呐与光学相机）在不同航次采集的数据因定位漂移和传感器外参偏差难以融合，亟需能同时纠正航次间偏移与航次内轨迹畸变的多模态建图方法。

Method: 以因子图优化为核心，联合估计载体轨迹、三维地标位置、传感器外参以及每个航次单独的全局对齐变换；将刚性航次间校正与局部轨迹形变结合，从而同时补偿航次间错位和导航累积误差引起的航次内畸变。

Result: 在加泰罗尼亚海岸真实数据集上验证，所有指标（像素精度、平均IoU等）均优于未优化和刚性对齐基线；像素精度相比未优化基线提升3.4%，相当于约14700 m²区域的语义标注改善；定性结果在明显轨迹畸变与航次间错位下仍保持声呐与光学地图的一致配准。

Conclusion: 该框架能从异构水下探测数据生成连贯的多模态海底地图，展示了多航次、多模态联合优化在水下测绘中的潜力。

Abstract: Accurate seafloor mapping is essential for marine science, archaeology, and environmental monitoring. However, integrating data from different sensors, such as side-scan sonar and optical cameras, collected across separate survey sessions, remains challenging due to positioning drift and sensor offsets. This paper presents a multi-session, multimodal underwater mapping framework based on factor graph optimization. The method jointly optimizes vehicle trajectories, 3D landmark positions, sensor extrinsics, and per-session global alignment transformations. By combining rigid inter-session corrections with local trajectory deformations, it compensates for both inter-session offsets and intra-session distortions from accumulated navigation errors. The proposed methodology was validated on real-world datasets collected along the Catalan coast. Results show measurable improvements in map consistency over both unoptimized and rigid-alignment baselines across all metrics, including Pixel Accuracy and mean Intersection over Union. The method achieves a 3.4% improvement in pixel accuracy over the unoptimized baseline, corresponding to improved semantic labelling across approximately 14700 $\text{m}^2$ of mapped area. Qualitative results further show consistent co-registration between sonar and optical maps, even in the presence of significant trajectory distortions and inter-session misalignments. These findings demonstrate the potential of the proposed framework to generate coherent multimodal seafloor maps from heterogeneous underwater surveys.

</details>


### [116] [Feedback-Modulated Harmonic Policies for Quadruped Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17946)
*Yixuan Jia,Steven Roche,Jonathan P. How*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将四足运动策略中每关节轨迹参数化为指令条件傅里叶级数，并用在线反馈网络调整关节偏移、谐波增益、频率与相位；仿真中与MLP隐层动态模态分解对比，Unitree Go2上实现3.67 m/s峰值速度与5.883 kg负载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流学习方法将观测直接映射到关节动作，步态周期性隐含于网络权重中，缺乏可解释、可调制的显式频率结构；作者希望用显式周期表示替代隐式策略，提升运动节律的可控性与可迁移性。

Method: 上下文网络根据指令（如速度）生成各关节傅里叶系数及逐步反馈网络权重；反馈网络输出在线修正关节偏移、各次谐波增益、基频与相位，构成指令条件傅里叶级数轨迹生成器。仿真中将该显式频率结构与直接输出关节目标的MLP策略隐层激活进行对比，并用动态模态分解（DMD）分析MLP rollout的激活模态。

Result: 谐波波形随指令速度改变频率与形状；DMD显示MLP激活主导模态集中于足高振荡频率及其二次谐波，说明其隐含周期结构。Unitree Go2实机上，仿真训练的谐波控制器录得机载估计峰值速度3.67 m/s，并在不同试验中承载最多5.883 kg附加载荷。

Conclusion: 显式傅里叶参数化与隐式MLP策略在周期结构上具有可比性，前者提供可解释、可在线调制的频率表示，并能sim-to-real迁移到四足硬件完成高速与负载任务。

Abstract: Learned quadruped locomotion policies commonly map observations directly to joint-level actions, leaving the periodic structure of locomotion implicit in the policy. We investigate an alternative representation in which each joint trajectory is expressed as a command-conditioned Fourier series and modified online using feedback from the robot state. A context network generates the Fourier coefficients and the weights of a per-step feedback network, whose outputs adjust joint offsets, harmonic gains, frequency, and phase during execution. In simulation, we examine this explicit frequency structure alongside the hidden activations of an MLP policy that directly outputs joint targets. The harmonic waveforms change frequency and shape with commanded speed. Dynamic mode decomposition of selected MLP rollouts reveals dominant activation modes near the foot-height oscillation frequency and its second harmonic, showing periodic structure without an explicit Fourier generator. On a Unitree Go2, the simulation-trained harmonic controller records a provisional onboard-estimated peak speed of 3.67 meter per second and carries added loads up to 5.883 kilogram in separate trials.

</details>


### [117] [Causal-History Test-Time Scaling for Failure Recovery in Autoregressive World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18016)
*Lin Li,Long Chen,Kwunhang,Wong,Jiaming Lei,Song Jin,Shucheng Du,Chuhan Zhang,Songchen Ma,Weihao Zhang,Jun Xiao,Kwang-Ting,Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出无训练的测试时扩展框架，通过因果历史的三阶段恢复（进度触发、历史前缀恢复、假设验证）提升世界-动作模型的失败恢复能力，仿真与真机实验均取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型（WAM）主要用成功轨迹训练，一旦真实执行偏离学到的动力学便容易失败；自回归WAM中错误会进入因果历史并持续污染后续预测，缺乏显式失败恢复机制。

Method: 将失败恢复重构为对因果历史的测试时扩展，分解为"何时、何处、哪一种"三个耦合决策：1) 进度感知恢复触发器，检测持续无进展并在状态允许干预时触发；2) 历史前缀恢复，定位不可靠历史后缀，检索与当前物理状态匹配的历史锚点，从保留前缀重建因果KV状态并以最新真实观测为条件；3) 假设验证，比较完整历史、恢复前缀、完全重置三种假设诱导的未来，提交支持度最高者。全程无需额外训练。

Result: 在仿真与真机操作场景中任务成功率均有一致提升，消融实验验证了各恢复阶段的贡献。

Conclusion: 以测试时扩展替代重训练来解决WAM的失败恢复是可行且有效的，因果历史的选择与管理是自回归具身策略鲁棒性的关键杠杆。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for robot manipulation by jointly modeling future visual dynamics and robot actions. However, existing WAMs are trained predominantly on successful trajectories, making them prone to failure when real-world execution diverges from the learned dynamics. This issue is amplified in autoregressive WAMs, where execution errors become part of the causal history and continue to influence subsequent predictions. To this end, we introduce \method{}, a training-free framework that reformulates failure recovery as \emph{test-time scaling over causal histories}. This formulation decomposes recovery into three coupled decisions: \emph{when} to revise the causal history, \emph{where} to recover a reliable history prefix, and \emph{which} history configuration best supports subsequent execution. Specifically, \method{} realizes these decisions through three stages: 1) \textbf{Progress-Aware Recovery Trigger} detects persistent non-progress and triggers recovery only when the current execution state permits intervention; 2) \textbf{History-Prefix Recovery} identifies the unreliable history suffix, retrieves a historical anchor matching the current physical state, and reconstructs the causal KV state from the retained prefix while conditioning on the latest real observation; and 3) \textbf{Hypothesis Verification} compares the future continuations induced by complete-history, recovered-prefix, and full-reset hypotheses, and commits the best-supported hypothesis. Experiments in both simulated and real-world manipulation settings demonstrate consistent improvements in task success, while ablations confirm the contribution of each recovery stage.

</details>


### [118] [Online Multimodal Workload Assessment in Contact-Rich Physical Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18031)
*Yanyi Chen,Fan Yang,Min Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出在线多模态接触丰富pHRI交互负荷评估框架，融合力旋量、TCP运动学与皮肤电导，用路径曲率与任务进度调权，以瞳孔直径为独立参考做LOSO验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的人机物理交互对物理交互、运动调节与生理响应提出时变需求，需要连续评估"交互负荷"；现有方法多为静态或黑箱学习式评估，缺乏可解释性与权重规则透明度。

Method: 将交互力旋量、平面TCP运动学与皮肤电导水平(SCL)整合为4个可解释的负荷相关因子；依据路径曲率（反映运动需求变化）与任务进度（反映生理缓慢漂移）自适应调整各因子相对权重；在24名被试×18种温度/噪声/照度组合×2种导纳控制模式下评估，采用严格LOSO，以标准化瞳孔直径PD_z为独立生理参考，与固定权重变体及代表性学习式SOTA基线对比。

Result: 队列平均30 s分块Spearman相关ρ30=0.308，24名被试中23名呈正相关；总体性能与SOTA学习基线相当，优于对应固定权重形式，且在两种导纳控制模式下表现一致。

Conclusion: 该框架在保持评估过程透明（显式负荷因子+明确加权规则）的同时达到与学习式基线相当的性能，支持接触丰富pHRI中可解释的连续交互负荷评估。

Abstract: Contact-rich physical human--robot interaction (pHRI) imposes time-varying demands associated with physical interaction, motor regulation, and physiological response, motivating continuous assessment of interaction workload. This paper presents an online multimodal assessment framework that integrates interaction wrench, planar tool-center-point (TCP) kinematics, and skin conductance level (SCL) into four interpretable workload-related factors. Their relative contributions are adjusted using path curvature to reflect changes in motion demand and task progression to account for gradual physiological variation over time. The framework was evaluated with 24 participants across 18 controlled combinations of temperature, acoustic noise, and illuminance under two admittance-control modes. Strict leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) evaluation used standardized pupil diameter ($\mathrm{PD}_z$) as an independent physiological reference and included comparisons with static variants and representative state-of-the-art learning-based baselines. The proposed framework achieves a cohort-mean $30\,\mathrm{s}$ block-wise Spearman correlation of $\rho_{30}=0.308$ with the physiological reference, with positive subject-level correspondence in 23 of 24 participants. Its overall performance is comparable to the state-of-the-art learning-based baseline. At the same time, our framework keeps the assessment process transparent through explicit workload-related factors and defined weighting rules, while outperforming the corresponding fixed-weight formulation. The framework also maintains consistent performance across the two tested admittance-control modes. These results support a transparent and interpretable approach to continuous interaction workload assessment in contact-rich pHRI.

</details>


### [119] [Geometric Shortcuts for Complex Trunk Postures: Dual-Helicity Coupling Enables Low-Dimensional Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18050)
*Huishi Huang,Danlu Chen,Matteo Lo Preti,Jun Liu,Marcelo H. Ang Jr.,Cecilia Laschi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出象鼻复杂姿态部分源于对侧螺旋肌肉的"双旋向耦合"这一低维几何捷径，用简化软体机器人原型仅调两个几何参数即复现丰富象鼻姿态，暗示双层控制架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 象鼻能产生高度复杂姿态，难以仅用精细的分段肌肉激活解释，需要更简洁的机制性解释。

Method: 理论假设：相反手性的斜向肌肉之间形成双螺旋耦合，构成低维几何捷径；在简化软体机器人原型上以两个几何参数为控制变量生成姿态库。

Result: 仅变化两个几何参数即可生成广泛的类象鼻姿态，表明高维姿态空间可由低维几何耦合有效覆盖。

Conclusion: 支持"双层控制"架构：低维几何耦合层+局部激活层，对连续体机器人设计与生物力学假说均有启示。

Abstract: How do elephant trunks generate complex postures without relying solely on fine segmental activation? We propose that part of this complexity arises from a low-dimensional geometric shortcut: dual-helicity coupling between opposite-handed oblique muscles. In a simplified soft-robotic prototype, varying only two geometric parameters generates a broad library of elephant-like postures, suggesting a dual-layer control architecture with implications for continuum robot design and biological hypotheses.

</details>


### [120] [CLASP: A Cluster-Level Autonomous Selective Picking Robot with a Soft Rolling-Band Gripper for Fresh-Market Blueberry Harvesting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18051)
*Yixuan Xia,Yilin Cai,Natalia Belen Espinoza,Changying Li,Zilfina Rubio Ames,Xin Zhang,Yue Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CLASP集群级自主选择性采摘机器人及软体主动滚带夹爪（SARB-Gripper），通过双带包络、滚动果实并在力闭环调节下依次摘下成熟果实，配合全局-局部感知流程实现蓝莓集群选择性采摘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲜食蓝莓采摘劳动密集且昂贵；跨行机械采收快但无选择性，会擦伤混熟果实并只能进入加工市场；现有选择性采摘机器人多针对单果而非果实集群，对小型密集簇生蓝莓效率低。

Method: 1）软体主动滚带夹爪：两条柔性带包络果实集群并相对果实滚动，顺序摘下成熟果实；2）力闭环调节牵拉力，使施加载荷低于未成熟果实脱落阈值；3）全局-局部感知：手眼外置相机做全局果簇检测与目标选择，眼在手相机做局部定位与果簇朝向估计；4）现场测量验证成熟/未成熟果实的脱落力分离。

Result: 现场测量确认成熟与未成熟果实存在明显的脱落力分离；SARB-Gripper对指令牵拉力的复现误差在3.7%以内；端到端田间试验中CLASP自主抓取25个果簇中的23个（92%）；单台组件成本约3326美元。

Conclusion: CLASP以较低成本实现了集群级选择性采摘，为鲜食蓝莓提供了一种可扩展的选择性采收方案。

Abstract: Fresh-market blueberries require selective, gentle picking, which is labor-intensive and expensive. Over-the-row machine harvesters are fast but non-selective, bruising mixed-ripeness fruit and limiting yield to the processing market. Selective robotic harvesters typically target individual fruits rather than fruit clusters, which limits harvesting efficiency for small, densely clustered blueberries. This paper presents CLASP, a Cluster-Level Autonomous Selective Picking robot with a Soft Active Rolling-Band Gripper (SARB-Gripper). Two compliant bands envelop the cluster and roll against the fruit, drawing mature berries off in sequence, while closed-loop regulation of the pulling force keeps the applied load below the immature detachment threshold. A global-to-local perception pipeline pairs an eye-to-hand camera for global cluster detection and target selection with an eye-in-hand camera for local localization and cluster orientation estimation. Field measurements confirm a clear detachment-force separation between mature and immature fruit, and the SARB-Gripper reproduces a commanded pulling force to within \SI{3.7}{\percent}, enabling selective harvesting at the cluster level. In end-to-end field trials, CLASP autonomously grasped 23 of 25 presented clusters (\SI{92}{\percent}). With the component cost of approximately \$3326 per unit, CLASP offers a scalable approach to selective cluster-level harvesting for fresh-market blueberries.

</details>


### [121] [An Efficient Algorithm for Minimum-Pressure Growth Planning of Vine Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18070)
*Andres C. Torres,Tobia Marcucci,Elliot W. Hawkes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出针对藤蔓机器人（vine robot）绕过多面体障碍的最小生长压力路径规划方法，通过推导任意形状藤蔓机器人的生长压力方程，证明最优解为分段线性路径，并将问题转化为时变权重最短路问题，用改进Dijkstra算法求解，2D全局最优、3D近似最优，并通过仿真与硬件实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 藤蔓机器人依靠从尖端生长来穿越杂乱环境，但其"生长规划"（即寻找最优生长路径）研究极少；且现有规划器忽略跟随给定路径所需的生长压力，压力过高会导致机器人爆裂。因此需要同时考虑几何路径与生长压力约束的规划方法。

Method: 1）推导适用于任意形状藤蔓机器人的生长压力方程；2）据此证明总存在一条最小压力路径，该路径分段线性且仅在障碍物上的特定点处弯曲；3）将该观察用于把生长规划问题归约为带时变权重的最短路径问题；4）用改进的Dijkstra算法高效求解，2D保证全局最优，3D为近似解且误差随离散化参数趋于零而消失。

Result: 数值仿真表明方法具有较快的速度与良好的可扩展性；硬件实验验证了算法在真实藤蔓机器人上的有效性；并开源了高性能Python实现VinePlanner。

Conclusion: 生长压力是藤蔓机器人路径规划中不可忽略的关键约束；将压力最小化路径规划归约为时变权重最短路问题，可在2D获得全局最优、3D获得可控精度的近似解，兼顾效率与可扩展性，并具备实际硬件可行性。

Abstract: Vine robots navigate cluttered environments by extending from their tip. Although their ability to operate in such environments has been extensively demonstrated, little work has addressed growth planning, i.e., finding optimal growth paths. Moreover, existing planners do not account for the growth pressure necessary to follow a given path, which can cause the robot to burst when it is too high. In this paper, we address the problem of finding minimum-pressure paths for vine robots growing around polytopic obstacles. We propose an efficient algorithm that is guaranteed to find globally optimal solutions in 2D and approximate solutions in 3D, with an error that vanishes as a discretization parameter approaches zero. First, we derive a growth pressure equation for vine robots of arbitrary shape, which we use to show that there always exists a minimum-pressure path that is piecewise-linear and can bend only at specific points on the obstacles. We then leverage this observation to reduce the growth-planning problem to a shortest-path problem with time-dependent weights, which we efficiently solve using a modified Dijkstra's algorithm. We demonstrate the speed and scalability of our approach through numerical simulations. We also validate our algorithm with hardware experiments and provide an open-source and high-performance implementation in the Python package, VinePlanner: this https URL .

</details>


### [122] [Characterizing Refraction-Induced Ranging Bias in Underwater Collaborative Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18073)
*Timothy Kogucki,Alan Papalia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作通过仿真量化折射引起的声学测距偏差对公里级多智能体协同定位的影响，发现声速梯度剧烈区域轨迹退化显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体测距辅助导航是解决大尺度水下定位的有力方案，其精度高度依赖测距质量。声速变化导致声线折射弯曲，而标准传感器融合管线为算法简洁性假设直线传播，使测距被系统性偏长。然而该偏差在公里尺度多智能体协同定位中的影响尚属空白。

Method: 构建仿真实验：多个智能体在公里尺度作业；用HYCOM再分析数据重建真实海洋条件，用射线追踪生成含折射的测距，再用集中式多智能体因子图估计器量化测距偏差对轨迹估计的影响。

Result: 初步结果表明，折射引起的偏差可导致估计轨迹显著退化，尤其在声速梯度陡峭区域；并开源了仿真环境以支持后续研究。

Conclusion: 标准直线传播假设在水下多智能体协同定位中引入系统性测距偏差，公里尺度下不可忽略，需在传感融合中显式建模折射效应。

Abstract: This work studies how refraction-induced bias on acoustic ranging affects multi-agent collaborative localization in a range of oceanographic conditions and spatial scales. While multi-agent range-aided navigation, which uses range measurements to either fixed infrastructure or other agents, is a promising solution to the challenges of large-scale underwater localization, its accuracy depends strongly on the quality of range measurements. Sound speed variability induces refraction (bending) of acoustic rays, yet, for algorithmic tractability, standard sensor fusion pipelines assume straight-line propagation. This refraction systematically biases range measurements to be longer than the straight-line assumption predicts. However, the effects of this bias on multi-agent collaborative localization on kilometer scales remains unexplored. We present a series of simulated experiments with several agents operating over kilometer scales. The simulation uses HYCOM reanalysis data to recreate realistic oceanographic conditions, ray tracing to generate refraction-informed ranges, and a centralized multi-agent factor graph estimator to quantify the resulting measurement bias on estimated trajectories. Preliminary results indicate that refraction-induced bias can induce significant degradation of estimated trajectories, particularly in regions with sharp sound-speed gradients. We also share the simulation environment to support further studies this https URL .

</details>


### [123] [Not All Layers Need Tuning: Diagnosing and Directing Adaptation in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18084)
*Shahram Najam Syed,Arthur Jakobsson,Prayuj Sachdev,Jeffrey Ichnowski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"观察-诊断-分配-适配"流水线，用无标签目标观测估计VLA各区域适配代价，据此分配变秩LoRA并冻结已校准区域，在五个VLA架构与真实xArm-7上以极少可训练参数达到或超越均匀LoRA与全量微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调VLA到新部署环境代价高昂，而现有方法普遍对所有网络区域施以等容量适配器，隐含假设"各区域需同等调整"。作者希望检验该假设是否成立，并利用区域间适配代价的结构性差异来降低适配成本。

Method: 在五个架构各异的VLA（OpenVLA-OFT、π0、SmolVLA、DTP、Octo，93M-7B）上，以区域隔离微调下的归一化参数位移度量各区域适配代价，发现外观偏移集中于视觉编码器、指令偏移集中于语言主干、新物体偏移集中于视觉编码器与动作头。据此提出流水线：仅用10个无标签目标观测，无参考的梯度与MC Dropout信号结合对缓存源参考的CKA分数估计各区域代价；分配器在参数预算下将估计转为变秩LoRA并冻结校准良好的区域；再用标准LoRA微调。

Result: 诊断在单次部署内区域排序的中位Spearman为0.91；在LIBERO与CALVIN上所有测试预算下分配方案匹配或超越均匀LoRA。物理xArm-7上，指令措辞偏移下以全量微调0.04%的可训练参数达到相当水平；五个未重训的留存场景中领先所有基线，30次rollout成功11-23次，最强参数高效基线8-18次，全量微调仅2-11次。

Conclusion: VLA的适配代价具有足够结构，可在微调开始前被测量并用于指导参数分配，从而实现高参数效率且跨架构、跨场景泛化的适配。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a Vision-Language-Action (VLA) model for a new deployment environment is expensive, yet most methods apply uniform-capacity adapters to every network region as if every region requires equal adjustment. This paper tests that assumption on five architecturally diverse VLAs (OpenVLA-OFT, $\pi_0$, SmolVLA, DTP, Octo; 93M-7B parameters). Measuring per-region adaptation cost as normalized parameter displacement under region-isolated fine-tuning reveals an adaptation spectrum in which appearance shifts concentrate cost in the vision encoder, instruction shifts in the language backbone, and novel-object shifts in the vision encoder together with the action head, across all five architectures. To exploit this structure, we introduce a pipeline that observes, diagnoses, allocates, and adapts. From ten unlabeled target observations and without fine-tuning, the diagnostic estimates per-region cost by combining reference-free gradient and Monte Carlo Dropout signals with a Centered Kernel Alignment score against a cached source reference; the allocator converts the estimates into variable-rank LoRA adapters under a parameter budget and freezes well-calibrated regions; and standard LoRA fine-tuning trains the resulting adapters. The diagnostic ranks regions within each deployment at a median Spearman of 0.91, and the allocation matches or exceeds uniform LoRA at every budget we tested on LIBERO and CALVIN. On a physical xArm-7, the pipeline matches full fine-tuning under an instruction-wording shift with 0.04% of its trainable parameters, and on five held-out scenes evaluated without retraining it leads every baseline, with 11-23 successes of 30 rollouts against 8-18 for the strongest parameter-efficient baseline at equal or larger budgets and 2-11 for full fine-tuning. These results suggest that adaptation cost in VLAs is structured enough to measure before fine-tuning begins.

</details>


### [124] [ReRadar: Robust Radar Global Localization via Rotation-Equivariant Descriptor Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18092)
*Duc Manh Nguyen,Truong Giang Dao,Gia Nghiem Luong,Viet Trung Hoang,Anh Quang Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReRadar 提出基于可转向卷积的旋转等变特征与 NetVLAD 聚合的旋转不变毫米波雷达描述子，结合检索与地标匹配实现 3-DoF 全局定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扫描式毫米波雷达全局定位困难，主因是现有地点识别描述子丢弃了精确定位所需的几何结构信息，且旋转变化影响描述子稳定性。

Method: 使用可转向卷积网络（Steerable CNN）提取旋转等变中间特征，通过群池化与 NetVLAD 聚合生成旋转不变描述子，再将描述子检索与地标匹配融合以估计机器人 3-DoF 位姿。

Result: 目标数据集适配下：OORD Bellmouth Recall@1 99.37%，Mulran DCC01 Recall@1 91.44%、F1_max 80.99%，Boreas 降雪序列 Recall@1 99.38%；无目标数据集时跨数据集 OORD Recall@1 98.07%，与所评估的 SOTA 相当。

Conclusion: 旋转等变特征加旋转不变聚合的雷达定位方案在多数据集上取得高召回率与良好跨数据集泛化，是对 SOTA 具竞争力的雷达全局定位流水线。

Abstract: Global localization with scanning millimeter-wave radar remains challenging because place-recognition descriptors often discard spatial structure needed for accurate pose retrieval. We present ReRadar, a radar global localization pipeline that extracts rotation-equivariant intermediate features using steerable convolutional neural networks, forms rotation-invariant descriptors through group pooling and NetVLAD aggregation, and combines descriptor retrieval with landmark-based matching to estimate the robot's three-degree-of-freedom (3-DoF) pose. Across fixed database-query evaluations, ReRadar with target-dataset adaptation achieves 99.37% Recall@1 on OORD Bellmouth, 91.44% Recall@1 with 80.99% F1_max on Mulran DCC01, and 99.38% Recall@1 on falling-snow Boreas sequence. Without target-dataset data, the cross-dataset model reaches 98.07% Recall@1 on OORD, performing comparably to the evaluated state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [125] [Beyond Pixel Similarity: Task-Aware Evaluation of GAN-Based Synthetic Sonar Data for Robotic Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18100)
*Hannan Ejaz Keen,Muhammad Moazam Fraz,Karsten Berns*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文检验GAN生成的合成声呐图像在像素级图像保真度指标（SSIM、PSNR、MSE）与下游目标检测性能之间是否存在一致性，发现二者存在明显脱节，主张对合成传感器数据采用任务导向评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据可降低机器人感知训练数据的采集与标注成本，但生成保留下游感知相关特征的传感器观测（尤其声呐图像）仍很困难；同时现有评估通常依赖图像保真度指标，其是否能反映下游任务性能尚不清楚。

Method: 采用Pix2Pix条件GAN，配置四种不同感受野的判别器（PixelGAN、PatchGAN-16、PatchGAN-70、ImageGAN）；基于两个数据集的声呐图像训练；用SSIM、PSNR、MSE进行像素级评估；再以仅在真实声呐图像上训练的YOLOX-S、YOLOX-L、Faster R-CNN在生成图像上进行任务导向评估，且所有判别器配置使用相同测试样本与标注。

Result: 图像保真度与下游检测性能出现不一致：取得最优SSIM/PSNR/MSE的配置并未稳定获得最佳检测性能；PatchGAN配置虽未达到最高像素级相似度，却获得较强的下游检测结果。

Conclusion: 在该数据集与模型范围内，仅靠像素级图像保真度指标无法稳定刻画合成声呐观测的任务相关真实性，建议对面向机器人感知的合成传感器数据采用任务感知的评估方式。

Abstract: Synthetic data can reduce the cost of collecting and annotating training data for robotic perception, but generating sensor observations that preserve the characteristics relevant to downstream perception remains challenging, particularly for sonar imagery. In this work, we investigate whether conventional image-fidelity metrics adequately reflect the downstream perception performance of GAN-generated synthetic sonar data. We employ a Pix2Pix conditional generative adversarial network with four discriminator configurations characterized by different receptive fields: PixelGAN, PatchGAN-16, PatchGAN-70, and ImageGAN. The models are trained using sonar imagery from two datasets and evaluated using conventional image-fidelity metrics, including Structural Similarity Index (SSIM), Peak Signal-to-Noise Ratio (PSNR), and Mean Squared Error (MSE). To complement these pixel-level measures with task-oriented evaluation, YOLOX-S, YOLOX-L, and Faster R-CNN detectors are trained exclusively on real sonar imagery and subsequently evaluated on the GAN-generated images using identical test samples and annotations across all discriminator configurations. The results reveal a discrepancy between image-fidelity and downstream object-detection performance: the configuration achieving the best SSIM, PSNR, and MSE does not consistently yield the best detection performance. In particular, PatchGAN configurations achieve strong downstream detection results despite not achieving the highest pixel-level similarity scores. These findings suggest, for the datasets and models considered, pixel-level image-fidelity metrics alone may not consistently capture the task-relevant realism of synthetic sonar observations and motivate the use of task-aware evaluation for synthetic sensor data intended for robotic perception.

</details>


### [126] [Technical Report: One-Step Drifting Action Heads for GR00T N1.7](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18108)
*Xihe Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将GR00T N1.7的迭代扩散动作头替换为一步drifting动作头，动作头前向耗时从约45.3ms降至5.0ms、端到端从约70.0ms降至30.6ms，但LIBERO任务成功率系统性下降（Spatial 64.0±4.0%、Goal 52.0±1.0%、Long 26.0±2.6%），被定性为速度-成功率权衡而非改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一步动作生成可显著降低VLA策略推理成本，但其对闭环任务成功率的影响尚无定论，作者希望定量刻画这一权衡。

Method: 在GR00T N1.7上以一步drifting动作头替代迭代diffusion-transformer动作头，并引入面向异步chunk替换的overlap-conditioned扩展；多随机种子训练在两张A800上完成，在LIBERO上评测同步执行路径。

Result: 动作头约45.3ms→5.0ms，backbone+head约70.0ms→30.6ms；三个种子下成功率Spatial 64.0±4.0%、Goal 52.0±1.0%、Long 26.0±2.6%，种子间方差低，说明退化并非随机初始化所致。

Conclusion: 一步生成带来明显延迟收益但伴随系统性成功率下降，属速度-成功率权衡；可能原因包括确定性一步模式平均、依赖batch的几何估计、长开环chunk执行，以及同步LIBERO评测未覆盖异步overlap路径。

Abstract: One-step action generation can substantially reduce the inference cost of vision-language-action (VLA) policies, but its effect on closed-loop task success remains an open question. This technical report studies a GR00T N1.7 variant in which the iterative diffusion-transformer action head is replaced by a one-step drifting action head, together with an overlap-conditioned extension for asynchronous chunk replacement. All multi-seed drifting runs were trained on two NVIDIA A800 GPUs. On LIBERO, the action head reduces the mean model-forward time of the action head from approximately $45.3\,\mathrm{ms}$ to $5.0\,\mathrm{ms}$, while the measured backbone-plus-head time falls from approximately $70.0\,\mathrm{ms}$ to $30.6\,\mathrm{ms}$. However, this speedup is accompanied by a systematic reduction in task success. Across three drifting seeds, success is $64.0\pm4.0\%$ on LIBERO-Spatial, $52.0\pm1.0\%$ on LIBERO-Goal, and $26.0\pm2.6\%$ on LIBERO-Long. The low seed variance indicates that the degradation is not explained by random initialization alone. We report the result as a speed--success trade-off rather than an overall improvement, and discuss likely contributing factors including deterministic one-step mode averaging, batch-dependent geometry estimation, long open-loop chunk execution, and the fact that synchronous LIBERO evaluation does not exercise the asynchronous overlap path.

</details>


### [127] [A Comprehensive Review of Generative Physical Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18111)
*Satyam Gaba,Krutiksinh Rana,Siva Sai,Vinay Chamola,Dusit Niyato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 一篇关于生成式物理人工智能（GPAI）的系统综述，提出五类方法分类（RFM、VLA、LBM、DPM、WFM），分析其架构、互补关系与应用，并指出数据高效学习、sim-to-real、边缘部署与安全等方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型与物理实体结合催生了GPAI智能体系统，但该领域方法分散、缺乏统一架构视角与分类体系，需要系统梳理其共性基础、互补机制与瓶颈。

Method: 综述性研究，非提出新方法：构建五类方法分类法（Robot Foundation Models、Vision-Language Action、Large Behavior Models、Diffusion Policy Models、World Foundation Models），并分析各类模型的架构基础、互补协作模式（如WFM为VLA/DPM生成数据、RFM实现跨平台策略迁移、LBM提供运动先验）及典型应用场景。

Result: 通过自动驾驶、工业自动化、医疗机器人和人形系统等案例，归纳出显著性能提升，并总结数据高效学习、sim-to-real迁移、边缘兼容架构与安全框架等有前景的研究方向。

Conclusion: GPAI为IoT连接环境下的具身智能提供了统一框架，五类方法的互补整合是推动智能体与网络化传感器、执行器及边缘设备交互的关键路径。

Abstract: The integration of large-scale foundation models with physical embodiments has led to significant advancements in robotics known as Generative Physical Artificial Intelligence (GPAI). These agentic AI systems autonomously perceive, reason, and act in complex real-world situations. This survey comprehensively analyzes GPAI systems, focusing on their architectural foundations, current applications, and key limitations. We introduce a taxonomy of five distinct approaches: Robot Foundation Models (RFMs) for cross-platform skill transfer; Vision-Language Action (VLA) models for end-to-end multi-modal perception and control; Large Behavior Models (LBMs) for human-like movement generation; Diffusion Policy Models (DPMs) for diffusion model-based temporally coherent action generation; and World Foundation Models (WFMs) for physics-compliant simulation and data generation. We examine how these approaches complement each other: WFMs generate training data for VLAs and DPMs, RFMs enable cross-platform deployment of learned policies, while LBMs provide motion priors for natural behavior. Through examples across autonomous vehicles, industrial automation, healthcare robotics, and humanoid systems, we identify significant performance improvements and summarize promising research directions in data-efficient learning, sim-to-real transfer, edge-compatible architectures, and safety frameworks. These insights advance embodied AI for IoT-connected environments where intelligent agents interact with networked sensors, actuators, and edge devices.

</details>


### [128] [OpenDexGrasp: Open-vocabulary Task-Oriented Dexterous Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18117)
*Jiyao Zhang,Junhan Wang,Tianyu Wang,Zeyuan Chen,Anthony Bolten,Yitong Peng,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 OpenDexGrasp 统一数据与生成建模框架，用 OpenDexVerse（OpenDex-Scale + OpenDex-Align，按 C2A Recipe 组织）做双源监督，学习共享感知-动作隐表示，将开放词汇视觉-语言上下文与灵巧抓取动作生成耦合，无需单独的可供性到姿态推断阶段，可直接生成任务一致的灵巧抓取，并在仿真与真机上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧抓取合成虽能生成稳定、物理合理的姿态，但缺乏任务功能一致性；真实操作需要保留任务隐含功能的抓取。开放词汇、语言驱动的任务导向灵巧抓取需要同时完成语言功能意图推断、多视角视觉与几何 grounding、以及高自由度可执行抓取生成，缺少统一的数据与生成建模范式。

Method: 数据层面：OpenDexVerse 双源监督，按 Coverage-to-Alignment (C2A) Recipe 组织——OpenDex-Scale 通过自动抓取合成与视觉-语言标注提供大规模语义与几何覆盖；OpenDex-Align 通过人类遥操作与类别级迁移提供高质量具身对齐。模型层面：学习共享的感知-动作隐表示，耦合开放词汇视觉-语言上下文与灵巧动作生成；用可供性 grounding 与抓取生成作为隐空间上的互补监督，直接生成任务一致抓取，省去独立的可供性到姿态推断阶段。

Result: 大量仿真与真机实验表明：功能对齐性、物理可行性、对未见类别的泛化能力以及真实世界执行成功率均有提升。

Conclusion: 任务导向开放词汇灵巧抓取可通过统一数据配方与共享感知-动作隐空间建模实现端到端生成，避免了分离式可供性到姿态推断，并在仿真与真机上验证了功能一致性与执行成功率。

Abstract: Dexterous grasp synthesis has advanced rapidly in generating stable and physically plausible hand poses, but real-world manipulation requires grasps that preserve the function implied by the task. We study open-vocabulary task-oriented dexterous grasp generation, where a robot must infer functional intent from free-form language, ground it in multi-view visual observations and object geometry, and generate an executable high-degree-of-freedom grasp. We present OpenDexGrasp, a unified data and generative modeling framework for this setting. OpenDexVerse provides dual-source supervision organized by the Coverage-to-Alignment (C2A) Recipe: OpenDex-Scale offers large-scale semantic and geometric coverage through automatic grasp synthesis and vision-language annotation, while OpenDex-Align supplies high-quality embodied alignment through human teleoperation and category-level transfer. OpenDexGrasp learns a shared perception-action latent representation that couples open-vocabulary vision-language context with dexterous action generation. Affordance grounding and grasp generation provide complementary supervision over this latent space, enabling direct generation of task-consistent dexterous grasps without a separate affordance-to-pose inference stage. Extensive simulation and real-robot experiments demonstrate improved functional alignment, physical feasibility, generalization to unseen categories, and real-world execution success. Additional details and videos are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [129] [Fetch My Beer: Synthetic-to-real Hierarchical Policy for Smooth Pick-and-place](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18119)
*Yingyue Li,Chenyangguang Zhang,Ruida Zhang,Bowen Fu,Guangyao Zhai,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出合成到真实的层次化扩散控制框架，用于液体容器稳定运输，抑制晃动与溢出，在平滑性和动态稳定性上超越现有操作策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态敏感操作要求执行全程保持稳定物体动力学。液体运输面临三大瓶颈：流体仿真用于在线强化学习成本过高；人类遥操作引入致晃加速度，污染模仿学习数据；现有策略管线只优化任务完成而非动态稳定性。

Method: 合成到真实框架：可扩展数据管线合成抓取、用视觉语言模型过滤不稳定位姿、经流体仿真验证运输轨迹；策略为层次化结构——高层模块将语言与视觉观测映射为SE(3)控制目标，底层潜在扩散控制器先在紧凑潜空间规划，再解码稠密动作块，实现高控制频率。

Result: 大量实验显示，系统在运输平滑性与动态稳定性上优于当前最先进的操作策略。

Conclusion: 层次化扩散策略结合物理验证的合成数据，可有效解决在线仿真昂贵与遥操作数据缺陷问题，实现液体容器的稳定运输，为动态敏感操作提供可行范式。

Abstract: Many real-world robotic applications require dynamically sensitive manipulation, where success depends not only on reaching a target state but on maintaining stable object dynamics throughout execution. We study the stable transport of liquid-filled containers, where a robot must move objects to target locations while suppressing sloshing and preventing spillage. Unlike conventional pick-and-place, this task imposes stringent requirements on motion smoothness and trajectory-level stability, exposing clear limitations in existing systems. Specifically, fluid simulation remains too costly for online reinforcement learning; human teleoperation introduces unintended accelerations that induce sloshing during imitation learning; and current policy pipelines optimize for task completion rather than dynamic stability. We propose a synthetic-to-real framework coupling physically validated data generation with a hierarchical, diffusion-based controller. The scalable data pipeline synthesizes grasps, filters unstable poses via a vision-language model, and validates transport trajectories through fluid simulation. The policy is organized with a high-level module that translates language and visual observations into SE(3) control targets, and a latent diffusion controller that first plans efficiently in a compact latent space and then decodes dense action chunks, enabling the high control frequency needed for smooth and stable motion. Extensive experiments show our system outperforms state-of-the-art manipulation policies in transport smoothness and dynamic stability. Our project page: this https URL

</details>


### [130] [Prior Evolution and Task Alignment for Aerial Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18153)
*Weiliang Deng,Zhengyang Dang,Yao Mu,Ximin Lyu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出在解析式轨迹优化框架内引入学习：先学习轨迹先验，再用 CEM 迭代演化其初始化分布，并训练执行感知 Critic 从接触、抬升、完成等真实执行结果中评估轨迹成功率，其冻结能量可作为可微抓取代价直接塑造轨迹生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中抓取轨迹优化存在两处瓶颈：(1) 问题高度非凸、对初始化敏感，有限计算预算下难以获得高质量解；(2) 人为设计的数值目标函数只是成功抓取的可追踪抽象，无法完整刻画决定任务成败的因素。

Method: 解析规划器 + 学习增强：(a) 从已优化运动中学得轨迹先验；(b) 基于 CEM 的进化过程用部署优化器评估采样初始化，保留有利样本作为新监督，迭代更新先验；(c) 执行感知 Critic 从接触、抬升、完成等结果学习预测物理执行成功率；(d) 冻结 Critic 能量作为可微抓取代价，让执行数据直接参与轨迹生成。

Result: 仿真与真实世界实验表明优化可靠性、轨迹一致性以及抓取性能均有提升。

Conclusion: 学习可有效弥补解析式轨迹优化在初始化敏感性与目标函数抽象性上的不足，形成"优化—执行—再监督"的闭环，提升空中抓取的实际成功率。

Abstract: Aerial grasping is a remarkable capability exhibited by predatory birds, allowing them to capture prey through highly coordinated maneuvers in flight. Inspired by this capability, researchers have developed various formulations to reproduce such maneuvers through trajectory optimization. However, two limitations remain in practice. First, the resulting optimization problem is highly nonconvex and sensitive to initialization, making high-quality solutions difficult to obtain under a limited computational budget. Second, prescribed numerical objectives are human-designed abstractions that describe successful grasping through a limited set of mathematically tractable quantities and may not fully capture what determines task success. We investigate how learning can address these limitations within an analytical planner. Accordingly, a trajectory prior is first learned from optimized motions and then evolved through a CEM-based process that evaluates sampled initializations with the deployed optimizer and retains favorable ones as new supervision. An Execution-Aware Critic learns from contact, lift, and completion outcomes to assess whether the optimized trajectories are likely to succeed in physical execution. Its frozen energy can further serve as a differentiable grasping cost, allowing execution data to directly shape trajectory generation. Simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate improved optimization reliability, trajectory consistency, and grasping performance.

</details>


### [131] [Energy-Regularized Imitation Learning for Force- and Work-Aware Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18164)
*Toshiki Otani,Hiromu Taketsugu,Norimichi Ukita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将机器人关节力矩×角位移定义的机械功作为可微正则项，训练功预测器从状态-动作估计该值，微调RVT-2策略，12个RLBench任务功耗降2.1%、成功率略升0.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习策略通常只优化任务成功，不惩罚物理上低效/高能耗的动作；而仿真器中的能量/功是仿真端非可微物理量，无法直接作为策略梯度的正则目标。作者希望在不引入显式可微动力学模型的前提下，实现能量感知的策略微调。

Method: 定义关节空间机械功代理量（关节力矩与角位移的乘积/积分），据此训练一个可微的功预测器，从机器人状态与动作回归该功值；把预测器作为可微正则项，对预训练操作策略（RVT-2）进行微调；在RLBench上对涉及接触、铰接运动、放置、推、扫等12个操作任务进行评测。

Result: 微调后平均机械功由208.8J降至204.4J（降低2.1%），平均任务成功率由86.2%小幅提升至86.9%。

Conclusion: 以可微功预测器作为正则项的工作感知策略优化，可在无需显式可微动力学模型的条件下抑制物理低效动作，且能耗下降未以成功率为代价。

Abstract: This paper studies energy-aware manipulation as a physically grounded learning problem. We define a joint-space mechanical-work proxy from joint torque and angular displacement, and train a differentiable energy predictor that estimates this work from robot states and actions. The predictor converts a non-differentiable simulator-side physical quantity into a differentiable regularizer for fine-tuning a pretrained manipulation policy. We instantiate the framework with RVT-2 on RLBench and evaluate 12 manipulation tasks involving object contact, articulated motion, placement, pushing, and sweeping. The proposed fine-tuning reduces the average mechanical work from 208.8J to 204.4J (i.e., 2.1% reduction), while the mean task success rate also increases slightly from 86.2% to 86.9%. These results show that work-aware policy optimization can suppress physically inefficient motion without requiring an explicit differentiable dynamics model.

</details>


### [132] [LUMO: Designing Luminous Contact Morphology for Repeatable Whole-Finger Contact Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18165)
*Dong Ho Kang,Youngsu Ko,Luis Sentis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LUMO机器人手指：通过内置LED照射柔性硅胶垫，接触变形改变侧向出射光图案，使接触位置外部可观测；联合优化力学与光学性能，结合关节力矩实现法向力估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低阻抗机器人手指仅靠关节力矩无法区分"指尖小力"与"根部大力"，因为同一力矩可由不同接触位置产生；估计力必须已知接触沿着手指的位置，因此需要一种能从外部观测接触位置的结构。

Method: 在柔性硅胶垫中嵌入LED，接触使垫变形，手指侧向出射光图案随接触位置改变；同一结构同时承载接触载荷，故对截面（垫轮廓、刚性载体、侧向空腔）进行力学—光学双目标优化：力学上低预载时垫顺应、载荷增大时载体限制变形，光学上不同接触位置在侧面产生可分离响应。采用刚—柔接触仿真、光线追踪与多目标贝叶斯优化联合搜索。

Result: 在两种硅胶、6个接触位置、10 mm与30 mm球形压头下，优化后形貌将相邻位置分离度相对"重新建立接触"的变异提升15%–59%；利用已知LED间距由光学响应估计接触位置，结合关节力矩在931个样本上取得1.44 N法向力MAE；两指手抓取中多根指节同时出现局域侧向响应。

Conclusion: 通过将接触位置编码为可外部观测的光学信号并与关节力矩耦合，可在无分布式触觉阵列的条件下实现接触定位与法向力估计；力学—光学协同的截面优化是关键，方法具备向多指手集成的潜力。

Abstract: A low-impedance robot finger reports through joint torque how strongly it is loaded, but the same torque can arise from a small force near the fingertip or a large force near the joint. Resolving the force therefore requires knowing where along the finger contact occurred. LUMO makes that location externally observable. Embedded LEDs illuminate a compliant silicone pad, and contact deforms the pad so that light emerging from the finger's side changes in a pattern set by where the load acts. Because the same structure also carries the contact load, we optimize its cross-section, including the pad profile, rigid carrier, and lateral void, for two behaviors at once. Mechanically, the pad conforms under low preload while the carrier increasingly restricts further deformation as load rises. Optically, different contact locations produce separated responses on the finger's side. The search uses rigid--soft contact simulation, ray tracing, and multi-objective Bayesian optimization. Across two silicones, six contact locations, and 10- and 30-mm spherical indenters, the optimized morphologies improve neighboring-location separation relative to variation from re-establishing contact by \(15\)--\(59\%\). Estimating contact location from the optical response using the known LED spacing and combining it with joint torque gives \(1.44~\mathrm{N}\) normal-force MAE over 931 samples. In a two-finger hand, localized side responses appear on several links simultaneously during grasps.

</details>


### [133] [Characterizing Replay Retention Under Dynamics Shift in Model-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18167)
*Everest Yang,Skye Thompson,George D. Konidaris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究持续基于模型的强化学习中回放数据保留问题：变化幅度与"年龄-陈旧度 AUC"共同决定应否遗忘旧数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人动力学变化后需持续学习，旧回放数据在新动力学下会拖慢适应，但全部删除既浪费数据，又在动力学回归时造成损失。论文试图刻画何时应偏向近期数据、何时应保留历史数据这一权衡。

Method: 提出两个刻画量：变化幅度（change magnitude）与年龄-陈旧度 AUC（衡量转移数据年龄对陈旧/新鲜数据的区分能力）；在两种运动形态、两种基于模型的 RL 算法以及 Real-World RL 基准扰动上测试；并构建一个仅基于交互数据的陈旧度估计器，替代部署环境中不可得的真实标签。

Result: 遗忘陈旧数据在大的永久性变化后有益，但在动力学循环复现、旧数据重新可用时有害；回放保留策略取决于变化幅度与动力学演化方式；所提出的估计器可在无真值标签情况下提供选择回放策略所需量。

Conclusion: 回放数据保留应依据变化幅度和动力学演化动态决策，而非单一固定策略；可从交互数据估计陈旧度以支持实际部署。

Abstract: Adapting to changes in robot dynamics requires learning from new data without discarding experience that may still be useful. In continual model-based reinforcement learning (RL), replay collected before a dynamics change can slow adaptation, while removing it unnecessarily reduces available training data and can be especially costly if earlier dynamics return. We study when recent transitions are preferable to the full replay history. Two quantities characterize this trade-off: change magnitude and age-staleness area under the curve (AUC), measuring how well transition age separates stale from fresh data. Forgetting stale data helps after large permanent shifts but hurts when dynamics recur and older data becomes useful again. Choosing a replay strategy therefore depends on predicting when older data will help or hurt. We test these effects across two locomotion morphologies, two model-based RL algorithms, and Real-World RL benchmark perturbations. Because ground-truth staleness labels are unavailable on deployed robots, we evaluate whether an estimator built from interaction data can still provide the quantities needed to choose a replay strategy after permanent changes. Our results show that replay retention depends on change magnitude and on how the dynamics evolve.

</details>


### [134] [Approximating High Dimensional Self-Motion Manifolds via Deep Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18169)
*Haitao Gao,Yang Song,Liao Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将冗余机械臂的自运动流形(SMM)视为给定末端位姿下构型条件后验分布的支撑集，从而把SMM恢复转化为"学习分布采样+聚类分离连通分量"的问题，方法维度无关，首次实现7R机械臂4维SMM的近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SMM恢复方法（零空间延拓、学习方法）都建立在"SMM是一条曲线"的假设上，无法推广到更高冗余阶数；而高效恢复SMM是可行且全局最优运动规划的基础。作者希望提出一种与流形维数无关、随冗余度增长无需改变网络结构的通用方法。

Method: 采用概率视角：SMM等价于给定目标位姿下关节构型的条件后验分布的支撑集。据此将SMM恢复分解为两步——从学习到的分布中采样，再通过聚类把不相连的连通分量分离开。该建模不依赖流形维数，冗余阶数增加时无需修改架构。

Result: 在1维SMM上，所提方法性能与最新的零空间延拓方法和学习方法相当；并且是首个能够近似7R机械臂位置任务中高冗余4维SMM的方法。

Conclusion: 概率式建模为任意维度的自运动流形恢复提供了统一框架，突破了曲线假设的局限，为高冗余机械臂的全局运动规划提供了新工具。

Abstract: Self-motion manifold (SMM) characterizes the geometric structure of the infinite inverse kinematic solutions set of a redundant manipulator at a fixed end-effector pose, and its efficient recovery underpins feasible and global optimal motion planning. Existing methods such as null-space continuation and learning-based methods are formulated around the assumption that an SMM is a curve, and do not extend to higher redundancy orders. We instead adopt a probabilistic view: SMMs are the support of the conditional posterior over configurations given a target pose, so that recovering it reduces to sampling from a learned distribution and separating its disjoint components by clustering. The formulation is independent of the manifold dimension and requires no architectural change as the redundancy order grows. In this work, we demonstrate that our method can approximate 1-D SMMs with performance comparable to the latest null-space continuation and learning-based approach, and that it is the first method capable of approximating highly redundant 4-D SMMs in a 7R manipulator for position tasks. Project website: \href{ this https URL }{ this https URL }

</details>


### [135] [TacBPM: A Tactile-conditioned Behavior Prior Model for Dexterous Reorientation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18174)
*Jie Yin,Wanli Xing,Zeyuan Zhao,Xuezhou Zhu,Zhijie Deng,Kaifeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种触觉条件化的行为先验模型，将多尺度"球体专家"策略蒸馏为潜在控制器，下游策略通过残差潜在动作复用固定触觉先验，从而减少从原始关节指令重新探索的成本，实现稳定灵巧手内重定向与手-臂任务并完成sim-to-real迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手内操作需要协调高自由度手指关节，进行间歇性、接触丰富的交互。策略不仅要跟踪目标姿态，还需发现既能保持物体稳定、又能适应几何形状、各向异性、位姿、接触与传感变化的手指步态（finger gaits）。直接从原始关节指令用强化学习探索代价高、样本效率低、稳定性差。

Method: 提出触觉条件化的行为先验模型用于灵巧重定向：将多尺度"球体专家"（multi-scale sphere specialists）蒸馏为潜在控制器，形成固定的触觉先验；下游策略以残差潜在动作（residual latent actions）复用该先验，替代从原始关节指令重新探索。先验以触觉-本体感受历史为条件，使潜在行为反映当前手-物交互状态。

Result: 在任意位姿迁移（各向异性物体）、指定轴旋转，以及手-臂"Grasp-to-AnyPose"任务（抓取、抬升、搬运并到达目标位姿，涵盖新工具几何与泛化摆放）上评估。实验表明该方法加速训练，在匹配的原始动作PPO近乎失败的条件下仍能获得稳定策略，并在手内与手-臂任务中实现成功的sim-to-real迁移。

Conclusion: 触觉条件化的潜在行为先验+残差学习为灵巧手内操作提供了高效、稳定的策略学习范式，缓解了高自由度接触丰富任务的探索难题，并具备真实机器人迁移能力。

Abstract: Dexterous in-hand manipulation requires policies that coordinate high-DoF hand joints through intermittent, contact-rich interaction. Beyond target-orientation tracking, such policies must discover finger gaits that preserve object stability while adapting to geometry, anisotropy, pose, contact, and sensing changes. We propose \method, a tactile-conditioned behavior prior model for dexterous reorientation. \method distills multi-scale sphere specialists into a latent controller and lets downstream policies reuse the fixed tactile prior through residual latent actions, reducing renewed exploration from raw joint commands. The prior conditions on tactile-proprioceptive history so latent behavior reflects the current hand-object interaction. We evaluate arbitrary-pose transfer across anisotropic objects, commanded-axis rotation, and an arm-hand Grasp-to-AnyPose task in which the robot must grasp, lift, transport, and reach goal poses for novel tool geometries and generalized placements. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed method accelerates training and enables stable policies where matched raw-action PPO remains near failure, with successful sim-to-real transfer in in-hand and arm-hand tasks.

</details>


### [136] [OmniRisk: Omnidirectional Trajectory-Risk Learning for Agile Quadrotor Dynamic Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18191)
*Yifan He,Yang Liu,Wenhao Zhao,Hai Lin,Deping Zhang,Mingze Ma,Fei Gao,Huan Yu,Zipeng Dai,Ziming Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OmniRisk是一个面向四旋翼无人机的全向预测式避障规划框架，通过离线学习轨迹级风险场并结合圆形卷积网络单次前向推理，实现障碍物数量无关的高效机载避障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机避障需在极短反应窗口内预判碰撞并选择可行机动。稀疏测距观测无法直接反映障碍物运动，而在线轨迹优化要么随障碍物数量扩展性差，要么在密集高速场景中以牺牲可靠性换取效率。

Method: 将LiDAR距离全景图、动态掩码与笛卡尔表面速度拼接为固定维度张量以联合表示几何与运动；构建与障碍物速度对齐的非对称风险场，强调接近、削弱远离交互，并沿预测相对轨迹累积以获得稠密监督；采用双分支圆形卷积网络在全向锚点格上单次前向预测候选基元的终端边界状态与动态风险，随后选择并用闭式重建生成所选基元，从而去除在线风险累积、使风险推理成本与障碍物数无关。

Result: 实现高效机载避障，真实飞行中在相对接近速度最高达15 m/s的情况下无需微调即可连续执行规避机动；代码开源。

Conclusion: 离线学习轨迹级风险、在线仅做推理与候选选择的范式可兼顾密集高速场景下的效率与可靠性，为无人机高速动态避障提供可行路径。

Abstract: Agile quadrotor avoidance of fast-moving obstacles requires anticipating collisions and selecting feasible maneuvers within short reaction windows. Reliable predictive avoidance remains challenging because sparse range observations do not directly reveal obstacle motion, while online trajectory optimizers either scale poorly with obstacle count or remain efficient at the expense of reliability in dense, high-speed encounters. We present OmniRisk, an omnidirectional planning framework that learns trajectory-level risk offline for efficient onboard evasion. A fixed-dimensional tensor combines LiDAR range panoramas, dynamic masks, and Cartesian surface velocities to represent geometry and motion jointly. We formulate an asymmetric risk field aligned with obstacle velocity that emphasizes approaching interactions and attenuates receding ones. Accumulating this risk along predicted relative trajectories provides dense supervision and discourages unnecessary hesitation after obstacles pass. A dual-branch circular convolutional network predicts terminal boundary states and dynamic risks for candidate primitives over an omnidirectional anchor lattice in a single forward pass, followed by selection and closed-form reconstruction of the selected candidate primitive. This formulation removes online risk accumulation along trajectories and makes risk-inference cost independent of obstacle count. OmniRisk enables efficient onboard avoidance, with real-world flights demonstrating consecutive evasive maneuvers at relative encounter speeds up to 15 m/s without fine-tuning. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [137] [WAVE-Go: World-Model Navigation with Adaptive Execution for Wheel-Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18193)
*Mingyi Li,Ji Li,Zhihao Ouyang,Yage He,Börje F. Karlsson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 WAVE-Go 图像目标导航框架，将世界-动作预测与可中断指令执行解耦：执行器自适应选择动作前缀，并在新观测使执行失效时取消待执行命令；用条件风险公式在累积失败预算下选择前缀，并为姿态/运动模式切换设置间隙、稳定性与任务证据校验。导航评测中 ID 成功率 74.1%、动态 OOD 63.3%，较最强基线提升 4.7/7.7 个百分点，碰撞由 4.4 降至 2.9 次/100m。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型可预测导航动作后果，但轮腿机器人在动态障碍或运动模式切换时，预测的动作序列在执行过程中常失效。因此需要一种既能利用世界模型预测、又能在线打断与取消执行的自适应执行机制。

Method: 分离世界-动作预测与可中断执行器；执行器在估计的累积失败预算约束下，通过条件风险公式自适应选择动作前缀并在观测更新时取消待执行命令；姿态与运动模式切换需通过间隙、稳定性与任务证据检查；与可中断的固定四指令执行进行对照消融。

Result: ID 成功率 74.1%（+4.7pp）、动态 OOD 63.3%（+7.7pp），碰撞从 4.4 降至 2.9 次/100m；相比固定四指令可中断执行，成功率 +4.0pp，重规划频率 -51.2%，碰撞率 -6.5%；消融显示运行时中断提升成功率、碰撞率与反应延迟，代价是更多重规划。

Conclusion: 自适应、可中断的执行能在导航性能与规划开销间取得平衡，为轮腿等混合运动机器人在动态环境中的世界模型驱动导航提供了可行范式。

Abstract: World models can anticipate the consequences of navigation actions, but predicted action sequences may become invalid during execution, especially when wheel-legged robots encounter dynamic obstacles or change locomotion modes. We propose WAVE-Go, an image-goal navigation framework that separates world-action prediction from interruptible command execution. Its executor adaptively selects an action prefix and cancels pending commands when updated observations invalidate execution. A conditional-risk formulation specifies prefix selection under an estimated cumulative failure budget, while posture and locomotion-mode transitions require clearance, stability, and task-evidence checks. In the reported navigation evaluation, WAVE-Go achieves 74.1% in-distribution success and 63.3% dynamic out-of-distribution success, exceeding the strongest baseline by 4.7 and 7.7 percentage points, respectively, while reducing collisions from 4.4 to 2.9 per 100 m. Compared with interruptible fixed four-command execution, WAVE-Go raises success by 4.0 percentage points while reducing replanning frequency by 51.2% and collision rate by 6.5%. Execution ablations also show that runtime interruption improves success, collision rate, and reaction latency at the cost of additional replanning. These results support adaptive, interruptible execution as a means of balancing navigation performance and planning overhead. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [138] [WholeBodyWAM: Learning Whole-Body World Action Models with Scalable Motion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18197)
*Bowei Zhang,Qiyao Zhang,Shuanghao Bai,Xinhua Wang,Meng Li,Yilei Wang,Leiwang Zhang,Jian Tang,Lu Zhou,Lei Sun,Zhengping Che*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WholeBodyWAM人形世界-动作模型，利用4K+小时异构人体/人形运动数据预训练语言条件Motion Expert，再经非对称MoT注意力与Video、Action Expert融合，实现可迁移全身运动先验并提升真实人形操作性能与数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形全身操作需要协调的全身动力学，但目标机器人上大规模轨迹采集成本高、难以扩展；而人类视频与人形平台的运动数据丰富，却无法直接作为特定本体的机器人动作。作者试图验证这类可扩展运动资源能否作为可迁移的预测先验用于人形world-action建模。

Method: 构建UniMotion-4K运动语料（超4K小时，来自人类视频、原生3D运动数据集与异构人形平台），并将其规范化至统一运动空间；预训练语言条件Motion Expert在无目标机器人动作监督下预测未来全身运动；后训练阶段通过非对称Mixture-of-Transformers注意力将Motion Expert与Video Expert、Action Expert集成，使预测的场景动力学与全身运动共同指导本体特定动作生成。

Result: 实验显示性能随运动预训练规模增大而持续提升，未来运动预测与下游任务性能均有改善，并可有效迁移到真实世界人形操作；预训练运动先验在有限目标机器人演示下显著提升数据效率。

Conclusion: 异构、跨本体的大规模运动数据可作为可迁移的预测先验，无需目标机器人动作标签即可预训练，并与视频、动作专家协同，成为人形world-action建模的有效规模化路径。

Abstract: Humanoid whole-body manipulation requires coordinated whole-body dynamics, yet large-scale trajectories from a target robot are expensive to collect and difficult to scale. In contrast, whole-body motion from human and humanoid sources is abundantly available, although such data cannot be directly used as embodiment-specific robot actions. This work asks whether these scalable motion resources can instead provide a transferable predictive prior for humanoid world-action modeling. We introduce WholeBodyWAM, a humanoid world-action model that learns whole-body dynamics from large-scale heterogeneous motion before target-robot training. We curate UniMotion-4K, a motion corpus spanning more than 4K hours from human videos, native 3D motion datasets, and heterogeneous humanoid platforms, and canonicalize these diverse sources into a unified motion space. A language-conditioned Motion Expert is then pretrained to predict future whole-body motion without target-robot action supervision. During robot post-training, the pretrained Motion Expert is integrated with Video and Action Experts through asymmetric Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) attention, enabling predictive scene dynamics and whole-body motion to jointly inform embodiment-specific action generation. Experiments show that WholeBodyWAM consistently benefits from increased motion-pretraining scale, improves future-motion prediction and downstream task performance, and transfers effectively to real-world humanoid manipulation. Moreover, the pretrained motion prior substantially improves data efficiency under limited target-robot demonstrations.

</details>


### [139] [Reinforcement Learning for Real-Time Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18207)
*Perry Dong,Kuo-Han Hung,Dorsa Sadigh,Chelsea Finn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Real-Time EXPO-FT：将大 VLA 的慢速动作生成与轻量编辑策略解耦，用 RL 微调实现满足实时控制的机器人策略，缓解推理延迟导致的观测陈旧与分布偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 VLA 模型推理延迟高，执行时的观测往往已过期，造成分布偏移，显著降低可靠性与性能。已有的异步策略执行多基于模仿学习，无法突破训练分布、进一步提升可靠性；因此需要能兼顾实时控制与 RL 微调的方法。

Method: 基于 EXPO-FT 框架，将慢而富有表达力的动作生成与快而反应式的动作编辑解耦：大预训练 VLA 依据行为先验提出动作块（action chunk），轻量编辑策略在最新观测条件下，根据状态变化快速编辑动作，从而做出反应式决策；以此实例化为 Real-Time EXPO-FT 这一面向实时 VLA 策略的 RL 微调框架。

Result: 在 Kinetix 基准上，10/10 个环境中使延迟策略取得优于延迟与非延迟方法的最佳性能；在四个动态真实任务（机器人递物、平衡球、桌上足球踢球、动态抓取）上，在线机器人数据上限 10 分钟，平均策略性能从 42% 提升到 97%，全程无需人工干预。

Conclusion: 该方法表明将延迟补偿与 RL 微调结合，能在极少在线数据下快速、样本高效地适应真实世界动态，并提升 VLA 策略的实时可靠性与部署可行性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning fine-tuning on top of large, pretrained Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offers promise for highly reliable robot deployment. However, because of their scale, modern VLA models suffer from high inference latency, so the observation used to select an action is often stale by execution time, creating a distribution shift that can substantially degrade reliability and performance. Prior work has explored asynchronous policy execution to reduce the effect of latency, but these methods are mostly built on imitation learning and offer no mechanism for moving beyond the training distribution toward higher reliability. We close this gap by enabling RL fine-tuning that meets the real-time control requirements of dynamic real-world manipulation. Our approach builds on EXPO-FT, a framework for sample-efficient, reliable VLA fine-tuning with reinforcement learning, and decouples slow, expressive action generation from fast, reactive action edits: a large pretrained VLA proposes action chunks using its strong behavior prior, while a lightweight edit policy performs fast, reactive decision-making by editing actions in response to changes in state, conditioned on the latest observation. We instantiate this as Real-Time EXPO-FT, an RL framework for finetuning real-time VLA policies. On the Kinetix benchmark, Real-Time EXPO-FT enables a delayed policy to achieve the best performance among delayed and non-delayed methods in 10 out of 10 environments. On four dynamic real-world tasks, robot object passing, ball balancing, table soccer kicking, and dynamic object picking, with online robot data capped at 10 minutes, Real-Time EXPO-FT improves average policy performance from 42% to 97%, all without human intervention, demonstrating rapid, sample-efficient adaptation to challenging real-world dynamics. Website: this https URL

</details>


### [140] [CANTABILE: Learning Expressive Dynamics for Robotic Piano Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18213)
*Woosik Kim,Wonhyeok Choi,Sunghoon Im*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CANTABILE：面向机器人钢琴演奏的力度感知框架，通过速度目标条件化策略、速度保真+起音覆盖双奖励，以及冻结基策略上的手指级残差微调，在RoboPianist的EXPRESSIVE-51子集上将Velocity F1从0.06提升至0.34。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
