Motivation: 机器人钢琴演奏是双手灵巧操作的基准，但现有进展几乎只用音符准确率（音高与起音时刻）衡量，忽略了音乐力度这一表达性演奏的关键维度，导致表达性既无奖励也无评价。因此需要一个显式建模并评估键击强度的力度感知框架。

Method: (i) 闭环score-to-contact：以即将到来的速度目标条件化策略，并将按键在起音时刻的角速度映射回MIDI velocity；(ii) 组合奖励：速度保真奖励与起音覆盖奖励耦合，防止通过漏弹难音来"提升"力度；(iii) 在冻结的力度感知基策略上叠加alpha缩放的纯手指残差，将击键强度自适应局部化，不干扰原有音符执行。

Result: 在EXPRESSIVE-51（RoboPianist中力度丰富的51首曲子子集）上，Velocity F1（在±8 MIDI速度容差内联合衡量音高、起音与强度）从基线的0.06提升至0.34，51首曲子全部改善，匹配音符的速度误差减少一半以上，与参考音频的log-mel距离降低8%；强度随机化训练还支持无需重训的运行时演奏强度控制。

Conclusion: 该工作将机器人钢琴演奏的评价从离散音符准确率推进到连续力度保真度，提供了一套可复用的力度感知策略学习与残差微调范式，并展示了运行时强度可控的实用价值。

Abstract: Robotic piano playing has emerged as a standard benchmark for dexterous bimanual manipulation, yet progress on it has been measured almost entirely by note accuracy -- which keys are pressed (pitch) and when (onset) -- leaving the musical dynamics essential for expressive performance neither rewarded nor evaluated. We propose CANTABILE, a dynamics-aware framework for robotic piano performance that (i) closes the score-to-contact loop by conditioning the policy on upcoming velocity goals and mapping each key's angular velocity at onset back to MIDI velocity, (ii) couples a velocity-fidelity reward with an onset-coverage reward, so that dynamics cannot be improved by omitting difficult notes, and (iii) refines a frozen dynamics-aware base policy with an alpha-scaled, finger-only residual that localizes strike-intensity adaptation away from nominal note execution. On EXPRESSIVE-51, a dynamics-rich 51-song subset of RoboPianist, CANTABILE raises Velocity F1 -- jointly measuring pitch, onset, and intensity within a +/-8 MIDI-velocity tolerance -- from 0.06 to 0.34 over the RoboPianist baseline, improves all 51 songs, more than halves matched-note velocity error, and reduces log-mel distance to reference audio by 8%. Intensity-randomized training further enables runtime control of performance intensity without retraining.

</details>


### [141] [UMI-Bridge: Action-Anchored Latent Alignment across Human and Robot Manipulation Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18232)
*Haiyi Liu,Jingming Ma,Ke Rui,Yuteng Wei,Yuan Ma,Yushen Zuo,Honglong Tian,Haoran Jia,Weitao Zhou,Jiawei Wang,Minglei Li,Shiyi Chen,Haiyan Mao,Jiaqi Zhang,Chun Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UMI-Bridge 以 UMI 为中间域，通过动作等价的潜在动作对齐，实现无机器人演示数据下的跨域表征对齐与 VLA 后训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人演示数据稀缺，人类操作数据（自我中心视频、UMI 手持演示）虽易采集，但视角、本体结构与动作监督的差异使得按操作动作而非视觉外观对齐表征十分困难。

Method: 以 UMI 作为中间域，用动作等价性替代像素相似性做对齐：UMI 动作监督将隐表征锚定到末端执行器运动与夹爪行为；同步的头-腕观测与成对的 ego-UMI 片段支持跨视角跨域对齐。在无机器人演示的人类数据上训练双视角隐动作模型（LAM），随后冻结其腕部教师与动力学模型，用于正则化 UMI 与机器人数据上的 VLA 后训练；共享的腕部接口在保留策略标准推理架构的同时提供训练期监督。

Result: 三个真机任务上平均成功率 91.7%，Naive Co-training 为 73.3%；在两个数据效率任务上，用 25% 机器人演示加 UMI 数据即超过全量 Robot-only 基线；另在两个无任务专属机器人演示、仅由 UMI 演示学习的任务上达到 85% 与 90% 成功率。

Conclusion: 动作锚定的潜在对齐可显著提升机器人学习的数据效率，并支持 UMI 到真机的任务迁移。

Abstract: Real-robot demonstrations are limited, motivating the use of human manipulation data collected without robots, including egocentric videos and handheld Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI) demonstrations. However, differences in viewpoint, embodiment, and available action supervision make it difficult to align representations across these sources according to manipulation motion rather than visual appearance. We introduce UMI-Bridge, which uses UMI as an intermediate domain to align representations according to action equivalence rather than pixel similarity. UMI action supervision anchors the latent representation to end-effector motion and gripper behavior, while synchronized head-wrist observations and paired ego-UMI clips support alignment across views and domains. We train a dual-view latent action model (LAM) on human manipulation data without robot demonstrations, then freeze its wrist teacher and dynamics model to regularize vision-language-action (VLA) post-training on UMI and robot data. The shared wrist interface enables this training-time supervision across both domains while preserving the policy's standard inference architecture. Across three real-robot tasks, UMI-Bridge achieves 91.7% mean success versus 73.3% for Naive Co-training with matched UMI and robot data. On two data-efficiency tasks, it surpasses a full-data Robot-only baseline using 25% of the robot demonstrations together with UMI data. It also achieves 85% and 90% success on two additional tasks learned from UMI demonstrations without task-specific robot demonstrations. These results support action-anchored latent alignment for data-efficient robot learning and UMI-to-robot task transfer.

</details>


### [142] [ForceDelta-VLA: Distilling Force-Conditioned ActionCorrections for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18242)
*Ju Dong,Yu Fu,Jian Chen,Yimeng Liu,Haocheng Zhao,Lei Zhang,Kaixin Bai,Liding Zhang,Diwen Zheng,Alois Christian Knoll,Angela P. Schoellig,Jianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ForceDelta-VLA通过“修正蒸馏”将力感知VLA的单次动作预测分解为可复用参考动作+力/延迟修正，仅用冻结教师模型的力条件与无力模式配对预测构造显式监督，异步调度回放训练后以轻量策略在参考动作间依力历史实时调整，9项接触密集任务平均成功率82.2%（ForceVLA 54.4%），成功试验峰值接触力降约26%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触密集型操作中力感知VLA有效，但现有做法把“任务级运动”和“依赖接触的调整”耦合在单次动作预测里，缺乏可分解为可复用参考动作与修正项的显式标签，导致修正难以学习与复用，接触变化时需重新生成整段动作块，响应迟缓。

Method: 提出ForceDelta-VLA修正蒸馏框架：用冻结教师模型的力条件模式与学得力无关模式的配对预测，构造显式的“力修正目标”；另设延迟修正目标补偿参考动作不匹配及参考状态变化。训练采用异步调度回放，复用执行时缓存的任务上下文。所得轻量策略依据近期力历史与机器人状态调整参考动作。

Result: 在9项单臂与双臂接触密集任务上平均成功率82.2%，对比原始ForceVLA基线54.4%；直接执行Stage-1 Temporal Teacher为70.6%。相对ForceVLA，完整系统在成功试验上将平均峰值接触力降低约26%（两种平台一致）。

Conclusion: 将力感知VLA动作分解为参考动作与显式力/延迟修正的蒸馏范式，可在不重新生成完整动作块的前提下快速响应接触变化，同时提升成功率并显著降低接触力峰值，验证了修正蒸馏在接触密集操作中的有效性与跨平台可迁移性。

Abstract: Force-aware Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies improve contact-rich manipulation, but typically combine task-level motion and contact-dependent adjustment in a single action prediction. Demonstrations provide no explicit labels for decomposing that prediction into a reusable reference action and a correction. We present ForceDelta-VLA, a correction-distillation framework that constructs an explicit force-correction target using paired predictions from a frozen teacher's force-conditioned and learned force-agnostic modes. A separate delay-correction target accounts for reference-action mismatch and the change in reference state. Training uses asynchronous schedule replay with the cached task context available during execution. The resulting lightweight policy adjusts the reference actions using recent force history and robot state, responding to contact changes between reference-action updates without regenerating complete action chunks. Across nine single-arm and bimanual contact-rich tasks, ForceDelta-VLA achieves an 82.2% mean success rate, compared with 54.4% for the original ForceVLA baseline. Direct execution of our Stage-1 Temporal Teacher achieves 70.6%. Relative to ForceVLA, the complete system reduces mean peak contact force over successful trials by approximately 26% on both platforms.

</details>


### [143] [Acting in Meters: Learning Metric Interactions for Precise Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18243)
*Lijie Wang,Zheng Lu,Yiming Wang,Heyang Yu,Kenghou Hoi,Bowen Hu,Di Cui,Tianyu Xin,Haoran Liao,Wanqi Zhong,Xingjie Fan,Yizhao Xu,Ziliang Wang,Fei Gao,Yiming Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出度量交互框架，用物体级ICT令牌与场景级MAIF场在物理笛卡尔空间中联合建模动作交互，两阶段适配提升多种VLA/WAM基线


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA与World-Action模型虽推进了语言条件操作，但动作、物体与场景几何间的度量关系常被隐式化；人类操作则结合语义理解与相对物体及环境的空间反馈

Method: 在统一度量尺度上建模物体级与场景级交互：物体级Interaction-Centric Tokens显式表示末端执行器相对被操作物体的位姿轨迹，并与动作联合去噪以提供物理接地监督；场景级Metric Action Interaction Field用动作与ICT查询注意力聚合度量点云特征，学习几何条件化的动作修正；采用两阶段适配

Result: 仅增加少量参数与训练步数即可提升多种VLA/WAM基线，LIBERO与RoboTwin 2.0成功率平均提升0.80与3.59个百分点，真实任务提升6.80个百分点，其分布外变体提升7.45个百分点

Conclusion: 将物体-场景度量交互显式化是提升语言条件操作策略的有效且轻量的途径

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models and World-Action Models have advanced language-conditioned robotic manipulation, yet often leave metric relations among actions, objects, and scene geometry implicit. Human manipulation combines semantic understanding of task-relevant objects with spatial feedback that guides hand motion relative to objects and their surroundings. Inspired by this, we introduce a metric interaction framework that models object-level and scene-level interactions in physical Cartesian space at a shared metric scale. At the object level, Interaction-Centric Tokens (ICTs) explicitly represent end-effector pose trajectories relative to manipulated objects and are jointly denoised with actions, providing physically grounded interaction supervision. At the scene level, the Metric Action Interaction Field (MAIF) uses action and ICT queries to attend to metric scene point-cloud features and learns geometry-conditioned action corrections. Through two-stage adaptation, our framework improves diverse VLA and WAM baselines with a small number of additional parameters and training steps. Experiments demonstrate average success-rate gains of 0.80 and 3.59 percentage points on LIBERO and RoboTwin~2.0, respectively, alongside gains of 6.80 percentage points on real-world tasks and 7.45 percentage points on their out-of-distribution variants.

</details>


### [144] [A3P5 NEMESIS Integrated Rover Design for Environmental Reconnaissance and Robotic Sampling with Reproducible Mobility Analysis and an External Data Machine Learning Calibration Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18245)
*Shafi Bin Sultan,Sabik Bin Sultan,Safwan Sadad*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: A3P5 NEMESIS 四轮漫游车设计研究：结合照片约束几何重建、子系统架构与可复现分析框架，明确区分原型证据与拟定功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将远程巡检、环境观测与轻量操作集成于单一可维护平台，并通过可追溯的工程设计与评估框架界定优先测量项与验证需求。

Method: 探索性检索10个查询的5,000条记录（4,897条DOI、1,212条元数据候选）；照片约束几何重建配置；静态力矩与倾覆几何模型；公开数据标定基准（7,344条小时观测、8个预测因子、时序划分、ridge回归、日块bootstrap）。

Result: 声明场景下每轮变速箱输出扭矩3.28 N·m（20°坡、等载荷分配）；2 kg前向载荷使几何前倾界由38.1°降至32.7°；标定基准在外部传感器阵列上取得0.502 mg/m³的留出CO RMSE（95%日块bootstrap区间0.435-0.569）。

Conclusion: 贡献为面向原型的可追溯工程设计研究与评估框架，集成野外性能尚待建立；结果均为设计筛选而非实测运行极限。

Abstract: A3P5 NEMESIS is a four-wheel rover intended to combine remote inspection, environmental observation and lightweight manipulation within one serviceable platform. This study develops a photo-constrained geometric reconstruction, a subsystem architecture and a reproducible analytical assessment while distinguishing physical prototype evidence from proposed functions. An exploratory search retrieved 5,000 bibliographic records across ten queries, yielding 4,897 distinct DOI records and 1,212 metadata candidates; selected primary studies and technical documents informed the design. The reconstructed configuration retains the carbon-pattern enclosure, independently steered wheel assemblies, folded manipulator, inclined camera mast and side sampling equipment. A declared 24 kg scenario predicts 3.28 newton-metres of gearbox-output torque per wheel on a 20-degree grade under equal load sharing; a separate static model shows how a 2 kg forward payload reduces the geometric front-tipping bound from 38.1 degrees to 32.7 degrees. These are design screens, not measured operating limits. A public-data calibration benchmark uses 7,344 eligible hourly observations, eight sensor/environmental predictors and chronological training, validation and test partitions. Validation-selected ridge regression achieves a held-out CO root-mean-square error of 0.502 milligrams per cubic metre, with a 95% daily-block bootstrap interval of 0.435-0.569 milligrams per cubic metre. This result concerns an external sensor array and cannot establish NEMESIS accuracy. The combined analysis identifies priority measurements, proposed control interfaces and mission-specific validation requirements. The contribution is a traceable engineering design study and evaluation framework for a prototype whose integrated field performance remains to be established.

</details>


### [145] [${M}^2$Tok: Multi-head Multi-codebook Discrete Action Tokenization for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18259)
*Chunpu Xu,Zhixuan Liang,Yuhao Zhang,Chi-Min Chan,Jessie Wang,Yang Xiao,Mengkang Hu,Xiaokang Yang,Yao Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出多投头多码本动作分词器 M²Tok，缓解 VLA 中连续动作离散化的重建瓶颈，降低重建误差并提升策略成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归语言模型已被扩展到图像与动作等多模态信号，但原始动作是连续信号，需先离散化为紧凑 token。现有离散动作分词器重建损失高，难以保留精确控制所需的细粒度动力学，形成"离散化瓶颈"，限制了下游 VLA 模型的性能上限。

Method: M²Tok 采用两项结构创新：(1) 将隐动作特征分解为多个 head，使模型隐式地将特定 head 与特定动作维度对齐；(2) 为每个 head 分配独立码本进行量化。利用多码本的组合特性扩展分词器表达能力，从而降低重建损失。

Result: 在 RoboTwin、Simpler-Env 和 3 个零样本真实世界任务上评估基于 M²Tok 的 VLA：重建保真度更优，VLA 成功率显著提升；消融实验验证了多头与多码本机制的有效性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过多头、多码本的动作分词设计可有效缓解离散化瓶颈，兼顾重建精度与策略性能，为 VLA 提供更优的连续动作离散化方案。

Abstract: Recent advancements have successfully adapted autoregressive language models to process multimodal signals, such as images and actions. Since raw action signals are continuous, effective tokenization is essential to map high-dimensional inputs into compact discrete tokens for autoregressive processing. However, existing discrete action tokenizers often suffer from high reconstruction loss, failing to preserve the fine-grained dynamics required for precise control. This ``discretization bottleneck'' significantly limits the performance ceiling of downstream Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. To address this, we propose $\mathcal{M}^2$Tok, a Multi-head Multi-codebook Action Tokenizer designed to minimize reconstruction error and enhance policy performance. Our approach introduces two key structural innovations: (1) we decompose the latent action features into multiple heads, enabling the model to implicitly align specific heads with distinct action dimensions; (2) we assign independent codebooks to each head for quantization. By leveraging the combinatorial nature of multiple codebooks, we significantly expand the representational expressivity of the tokenizer, leading to substantially lower reconstruction loss compared to previous methods. We evaluate the $\mathcal{M}^2$Tok-based VLA on the RoboTwin, Simpler-Env, and 3 zero-shot real-world tasks. Experimental results demonstrate our method not only achieves superior reconstruction fidelity but also significantly boosts the success rate of VLA models. Comprehensive ablation studies further confirm the effectiveness of the multi-head and multi-codebook mechanisms. Code is available at \href{ this https URL }{ this https URL }.

</details>


### [146] [Function-Preserving Data Generation for Zero-Shot Real-to-Sim-to-Real Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18293)
*Tianyi Xiang,Xupeng Xie,Jiahang Cao,Andrew F. Luo,Haoang Li,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种"功能保持"的Real-to-Sim-to-Real数据生成框架，通过约束引导的网格形变增广任务相关物体几何，并一致地迁移任务位姿与碰撞代理，实现接触丰富任务无需遥操作示教的合成演示生成与零样本真机部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人数据生成是扩展机器人学习的可行范式，但接触丰富任务要求精确的几何接口，标准形状增广会破坏关键接口，导致配合失配或穿模等无效接触关系，使下游交互不可行。

Method: 约束引导的网格形变（约束保持任务功能）增广任务相关物体几何；物理一致地迁移任务位姿与碰撞代理；仿真rollout中施加视觉域随机化；整体为无需遥操作源轨迹的Real-to-Sim-to-Real流程。

Result: 在真机与仿真实验中，方法在未见物体几何与多样视觉条件下对接触丰富及长时程任务展现出鲁棒泛化，并可零样本部署、无需真机微调。

Conclusion: 形状形变驱动的功能保持合成数据生成为接触丰富任务的可扩展机器人学习提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Robotic data generation is a promising paradigm for scaling robot learning without collecting large-scale real-world data. However, generating geometrically diverse yet physically valid data for contact-rich tasks remains challenging, especially when success depends on precise geometric interfaces. Standard shape augmentation methods often distort task-critical interfaces, resulting in invalid contact relationships, e.g., fit mismatches or interpenetration, rendering downstream interactions infeasible. To address these limitations, we propose a function-preserving Real-to-Sim-to-Real framework that generates synthetic demonstrations from reconstructed assets without teleoperated source trajectories. Our method augments task-relevant object geometries through constraint-guided mesh deformation, together with physically consistent transfer of task poses and collision proxies. Visual domain randomization is further applied during simulation rollouts, enabling robust zero-shot policy deployment without real-world fine-tuning. Extensive experiments in both real-world and simulation settings demonstrate that our method enables robust generalization across unseen object geometries and diverse visual conditions in contact-rich and long-horizon tasks. Our method provides a practical path toward scalable robot learning for contact-rich tasks via shape deformation.

</details>


### [147] [RAFAIL: Relationship-Aware Failure Detection for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18324)
*Loris Schneider,Edgar Welte,Rania Rayyes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAFAIL 通过检测任务相关实体关系（如夹爪-物体、物体-目标）上的点云关系表示异常来识别机器人操作执行失败，离线用 VLM 标注任务进度与关系重要性，运行时无需 VLM 推理即可预测，无需失败数据，在三个真实操作任务上取得 73.4% 平衡准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作中执行失败检测对可靠性至关重要。现有 VLM 方法语义评估准确但引入运行时计算开销；OOD 检测器易对无害的场景变化产生误报，而非真正与失败相关的偏差。因此需要一个既轻量又聚焦任务相关偏差的失败检测框架。

Method: RAFAIL 将 OOD 检测聚焦于任务相关的实体间关系。离线阶段，VLM 对成功演示标注任务进度与关系重要性，据此学习基于点云的关系表示（不依赖策略内部特征）。运行时，关系专属的 OOD 检测器评估这些表示，同时在不进行 VLM 推理的情况下预测关系重要性与任务进度。

Result: 在三个真实世界机器人操作任务上达到 73.4% 平衡准确率，优于最强的 OOD 与不确定性基线，且无需任何失败数据。

Conclusion: 将 OOD 检测限定在任务相关关系上可有效降低对任务无关场景变化的敏感度，实现轻量、无需失败数据的机器人操作失败检测。

Abstract: Detecting failures during execution is essential for reliable robotic manipulation. Vision-language models (VLMs) can assess task outcomes semantically but add runtime computation, whereas out-of-distribution (OOD) detectors may respond to harmless scene variations rather than failure-relevant deviations. We introduce RAFAIL, a framework for detecting execution failures during robotic manipulation. RAFAIL identifies failures by detecting anomalies in task-relevant relationships between entities, such as a gripper and an object or an object and its target. By focusing OOD detection on relevant parts of the observation, RAFAIL reduces sensitivity to task-irrelevant scene variation. Offline, a VLM annotates successful demonstrations with task progress and relationship importance, which are used to learn point-cloud-based relationship representations without relying on policy-internal features. At runtime, relationship-specific OOD detectors evaluate these representations while relationship importance and task progress are predicted without VLM inference. RAFAIL requires no failure data and achieves 73.4% balanced accuracy across three real-world robotic manipulation tasks, outperforming the strongest evaluated OOD- and uncertainty-based baselines.

</details>


### [148] [UAVs Meet Embodied Intelligence: Bridging Human Intents and Flying Dynamics Via Harnessing Physical-Digital AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18326)
*Yonglin Tian,Weiyi Wang,Houhua Lu,Xinyi Li,Yihao Wu,Jingyang Chen,Jianli Sun,Chengxiang Li,Yinuo Chen,Fei Lin,Tengchao Zhang,Jing Yang,Deyi Ji,Jian Di,Naiqi Wu,Yisheng Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出"UAV具身智能（UAV EI）"概念，并用5+5框架（五能力维度+五架构层次）系统综述无人机具身形态、感知、世界模型、规划、VLN、操作与协作等进展，指出长时程自主、预测式物理推理、测试时技能获取与能力自主演化四大挑战，主张以物理-数字AI智能体持续耦合作为系统级演化路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型、世界模型与AI智能体推动无人机自主性从任务专用感知控制转向可理解人类意图、推理物理后果、组织复杂行为的系统；领域缺乏统一的概念界定与架构视角，需要将"UAV具身智能"与其系统实现（EI UAV）区分并建立统一框架。

Method: 提出5+5框架：以五个能力维度刻画UAV EI，以五个架构层次（物理具身、通用认知、具身技能、外部交互、系统整合）刻画EI UAV；据此系统性综述具身形态、具身感知、世界模型、具身规划、视觉语言导航、具身操作与具身协作等方向的近期进展，并归纳关键挑战与物理-数字智能体耦合路径。

Result: 论文为综述/立场性工作，未提供新的实验验证或定量结果；主要产出是概念界定、5+5统一框架、按维度组织的文献梳理，以及对四类核心挑战与系统级演化路径的论证。

Conclusion: UAV具身智能是面向三维连续空间的具身智能重要分支；其通用化受限于长时程自主、预测式物理推理、测试时技能获取与能力自主演化；通过物理-数字AI智能体的持久耦合实现数字智能与物理感知、动力学、行动和反馈的闭环，是通向自适应、持续演化无人机自主性的系统级路径。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) extend embodied intelligence into continuous three-dimensional space, where perception, reasoning, physical embodiment, and action are tightly coupled through flight and environmental interaction. Recent advances in foundation models, world models, and AI agents are shifting UAV autonomy from task-specific perception and control toward systems that can interpret human intent, understand open environments, reason about physical consequences, and organize complex behaviors under embodiment and flight-dynamic constraints. We characterize this emerging paradigm as UAV embodied intelligence (UAV EI) and distinguish it from its system realization, the embodied-intelligent UAV (EI UAV). To provide a unified view of the field, we introduce a 5+5 framework that describes UAV EI through five capability dimensions and EI UAVs through five architectural layers spanning physical embodiment, general cognition, embodied skills, external interaction, and system harnessing. Based on this framework, we systematically review recent progress in embodied morphology, embodied perception, world models, embodied planning, vision-language navigation, embodied manipulation, and embodied collaboration. We further identify long-horizon autonomy, predictive physical reasoning, test-time skill acquisition, and autonomous capability evolution as key challenges toward more general aerial embodied intelligence. Finally, we argue that harnessing physical-digital AI agents, through persistent coupling of digital intelligence with physical sensing, dynamics, action, and feedback, provides a system-level pathway toward adaptive and continuously evolving UAV autonomy. Project resources are available at our project website and GitHub repository.

</details>


### [149] [GraphPoint: Semantic Entity Graphs and Point Trajectories for Compositional Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18358)
*Kang Luo,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 CoMani 基准与 GraphPoint 方法，研究机器人操作策略在子任务内组合泛化与跨子任务长程复用两个层面的指令依赖泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作策略难以泛化到演示之外，即使新指令仅涉及熟悉物体与行为；当训练中语言与场景强相关时，策略易学到固定的视觉—动作映射而非响应语言指令。

Method: 构建 CoMani 基准，通过受控数据划分、匹配初始场景与单语义因子扰动，抑制视觉捷径；提出 GraphPoint，将语义实体图与几何控制相连接，预测未来夹爪点轨迹并经机器人几何转换为动作，按语义角色组织夹爪与物体，并以动作类型与修饰词为条件，利用预测进度引导长程执行中的子任务切换。

Result: 在 CoMani 上的实验与消融表明，该方法在子任务内与跨子任务两个层面的指令依赖泛化上均有效。

Conclusion: 语义图与几何轨迹预测的结合可缓解语言—视觉相关性导致的捷径学习，为组合式指令泛化与长程任务复用提供了可行框架，代码将在 GraphPoint 释放。

Abstract: Robot manipulation policies often struggle to generalize beyond their demonstrations, even when new instructions involve familiar objects and behaviors. When language and scenes are strongly correlated during training, a policy can learn a fixed visual-action mapping rather than respond to the requested behavior. We investigate compositional reuse at two levels: within a subtask, combining familiar entities, action types, and action modifiers; and across subtasks, reusing learned subtasks in unseen long-horizon tasks. We introduce CoMani, a benchmark with controlled splits for evaluating both capabilities. Matched initial scenes and controlled changes to a single semantic factor encourage reliance on language rather than visual shortcuts. We further propose GraphPoint, which connects semantic entity graphs to geometric control by predicting future gripper point trajectories and converting them into actions using robot geometry. The framework organizes the gripper and objects by semantic roles and conditions their interactions on action types and modifiers, while predicted progress guides transitions during execution. Experiments and ablations on CoMani validate the effectiveness of our method for instruction-dependent generalization at both levels. Code will be released at GraphPoint.

</details>


### [150] [RecMorph: Topology-Guided Spatial Recurrence for Generalized Morphology Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18359)
*Quanrui Rao,Yong Liu,Xueming Xiao,Yingbo Luo,Kun Wu,Zhenyu Xu,Meibao Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RecMorph：拓扑引导的空间循环架构，将运动学树深度优先展开为形态序列，用共享双向循环变换实现跨肢体通信与表示变换，实现线性复杂度并在UNIMAL及四足平台验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用形态控制要求单一策略适配物理角色不同的肢体、协调全身运动并随体型增长保持高效；现有通信机制只能部分满足。

Method: 将运动学树深度优先遍历为形态衍生序列，沿序列用共享双向循环变换逐步变换肢体信息后再解码动作；辅以残差保留、RMS归一化与输入相关通道调制以稳定重复的空间变换，固定宽度深度下为线性token复杂度。

Result: 五个UNIMAL任务上平均最终训练性能最佳、FT上推理吞吐最高，可泛化到未见变体及最多30肢体；迁移到四平台四足设置后宏平均性能最佳，标称速度RMSE较专家MLP降低43.5%，单一共享策略完成40次Go1/Go2实物试验无跌倒。

Conclusion: 拓扑引导的循环变换为通用形态控制提供了有效且高效的通信机制，且从程序化身体迁移到实物机器人平台仍保持有效。代码与实验资源公开。

Abstract: Generalized morphology control requires a single policy to transform information across limbs with different physical roles, coordinate whole-body motion, and remain efficient as body size grows. Existing communication mechanisms address these requirements only partially. We introduce RecMorph, a topology-guided spatial recurrent architecture that uses recurrent sequence computation to jointly perform cross-limb communication and representation transformation. A depth-first traversal converts the kinematic tree into a morphology-derived sequence, along which shared bidirectional transitions progressively transform limb information before action decoding. Residual preservation, RMS normalization, and input-dependent channel modulation stabilize this repeated spatial transformation, yielding linear token complexity at fixed model width and depth. Across five UNIMAL tasks, RecMorph achieves the strongest mean final training performance among the evaluated generalized morphology controllers and the highest measured inference throughput on FT, while generalizing to unseen variations and bodies with up to 30 limbs. We further migrate representative generalized controllers from UNIMAL benchmarks to a four-platform quadruped setting. RecMorph achieves the best macro-averaged performance under nominal and high friction, reduces nominal velocity RMSE by 43.5% relative to specialist MLPs, and one shared policy completes 40 physical Go1/Go2 trials without falls. These results show that topology-guided recurrent transformation provides an effective and efficient communication mechanism for Generalized Morphology Control and remains effective when transferred from procedural bodies to physical robot platforms. Code and experimental resources are publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [151] [Decoupling Vision, Language, and Action for Efficient Multi-Task Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18374)
*Xiatao Sun,Chen Liang,Ziyao Zeng,Qian Wang,Haoyang Zhang,Yue Sun,Qiucheng Li,Daniel Rakita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出解耦式具身模型DEM:以微调DINOv3视觉编码器+冻结NeoBERT语言编码器+MeanFlow动作头替代十亿级VLM骨干,在18个仿真任务与3个真机任务上达到接近SOTA VLA的成功率,推理频率提升8-17倍,单次推理能耗降低6-15倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型将动作模块挂接在十亿参数级VLM上,并在每个控制步都付出VLM前向推理的开销。对底层操作策略而言该代价可能并不必要:VLM实际只提供视觉与语言嵌入,而当前独立视觉编码器与仅编码器语言模型在视觉嵌入与语言理解基准上已可比肩甚至超越大VLM。作者希望以受控实验检验"解耦组件"能否在成功率-延迟-能耗上取得更优权衡。

Method: 受控实验:固定专家演示、训练预算、任务集合与评测平台,仅变换解耦策略的视觉编码器、语言编码器与动作头,并与7个VLA基线对比。最终方案DEM = 微调DINOv3视觉编码器 + 冻结NeoBERT语言编码器 + MeanFlow动作头(单次前向即生成整段动作chunk)。评测含18个仿真操作任务(使用留出的语言改写指令与随机化场景)以及3个真机任务。

Result: 在留出语言改写与随机场景下,DEM的观测成功率与VLM骨干的SOTA策略相当(同一评测协议下);推理频率为其8-17倍,单次推理能耗为其1/6至1/15。真机任务上同样表现可比。

Conclusion: 在该任务范围内,现代解耦组件相较整体式VLM骨干提供了更好的成功率-延迟-能耗权衡;VLA未必需要每步调用大模型骨干,视觉/语言嵌入与动作生成可以解耦。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models attach an action module to a Vision-Language Model (VLM) with billions of parameters and pay for that backbone at every control step. For a low-level manipulation policy, this cost may be unnecessary: the VLM supplies vision and language embeddings, and recent standalone vision encoders and encoder-only language models now match or exceed large VLMs on visual embedding and language understanding benchmarks. We study this question with a controlled experiment. Holding the demonstrations, the training budget, the tasks, and the measurement platform fixed, we vary the vision encoder, the language encoder, and the action head of a decoupled policy and compare against seven VLA baselines. The study yields the Decoupled Embodiment Model (DEM), which pairs a fine-tuned DINOv3 encoder and a frozen NeoBERT encoder with a MeanFlow head that generates each action chunk in a single forward pass. On 18 simulated manipulation tasks with held-out language paraphrases and randomized scenes, and on three real-robot tasks, DEM achieves observed success comparable to state-of-the-art VLM-backbone policies under our evaluation protocol, while running at eight to seventeen times their inference frequency and drawing six to fifteen times less energy per inference. Within this task scope, modern decoupled components offer a better success--latency--energy trade-off.

</details>


### [152] [DistAL: Distance-based Advantage Learning for VLA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18392)
*Reece O'Mahoney,Ioannis Havoutis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DistAL 用嵌入空间距离作为奖励信号替代稀疏成功/失败奖励，训练出信息量更高的价值函数，从而提升优势条件化 VLA 策略的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有优势条件化 VLA 仅使用简单、低信息量的成功/失败奖励，价值函数无法区分"进度相似但质量不同"的状态，限制策略改进上限。作者受 OOD 检测方法启发，认为状态与成功分布的距离可提供更细粒度的价值信号。

Method: Distance-based Advantage Learning（DistAL）：在嵌入空间中以样本到参考/成功分布的距离作为奖励，替代二值成功/失败奖励，用于训练价值函数，再以优势条件化方式训练 flow-matching 策略。方法层面属于奖励塑形/价值学习的改进，非全新架构。

Result: 摘要称在多个仿真基准上验证，并在真实硬件上完成灵巧双臂操作任务，下游任务成功率相比基线提升；但未给出具体数值、任务数量或消融规模。

Conclusion: 用分布距离奖励可显著增强 VLA 优势条件化训练的价值表征质量，并在仿真与真实双臂平台上取得更高成功率，指向"高质量奖励/价值信号"是 VLA 迭代改进的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) have trans- formed the field of robotic manipulation in recent years by combining the semantic understanding of LLMs with the precise control of flow-matching policies. Advantage conditioning is a recent technique that iteratively improves VLAs by training a value function on deployment data and using this to train an advantage-conditioned policy. Previous works have only applied simple, low-information success/failure rewards, which leave the value function unable to distinguish states of differing quality beyond how far along the task they appear. Motivated by an exploration of out-of-distribution (OOD) detection methods, we introduce Distance-based Advantage Learning (DistAL), which, by using an embedding space distance as a reward, produces a more informative value function and subsequently a higher downstream task success rate. We validate our method on a series of simulation benchmarks and dexterous bi-manual manipulation tasks on real hardware.

</details>


### [153] [DetAug: Obstacle-Blind Trajectory Augmentation for Zero-shot Obstacle Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18395)
*Reece O'Mahoney,Moritz Zoellner,Ioannis Havoutis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DetAug 通过将"障碍无关"的增强应用于自由空间数据集的过境阶段并记录增强参数作为显式条件标签，在推理时采样标签批次执行碰撞代价最低的轨迹，无需训练时已知障碍几何即可实现灵活避障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作机器人大数据集以自由空间轨迹为主，难以泛化到含障碍的测试环境。已有两条路线各有缺陷：数据集增强需要提前获知障碍几何；推理期引导现有策略虽无需障碍信息，但灵活性受限。作者希望兼取二者优势而不继承其缺点。

Method: DetAug 对自由空间数据集的过境（transit）阶段施加障碍盲增强，保留物体交互段不动，并将增强参数记录为显式条件标签，使策略学会以标签为条件生成不同绕行轨迹。推理时采样一批候选标签，执行碰撞代价最低的轨迹。

Result: 在 SafeLIBERO 基准上，无碰撞成功率比次优方法高出 20 个百分点以上，标签空间选择相比同一策略上的引导方法提升 26pp。真实硬件上，推理期引导方法在大幅度绕行任务上失效，而 DetAug 在训练中从未见过障碍的情况下匹配或超过了障碍条件化基线。

Conclusion: 将数据增强与推理期选择统一在显式条件标签框架下，可同时获得训练期对障碍几何的免依赖与推理期的高灵活性，为自由空间数据集向含障碍环境迁移提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Policies for robotic manipulation are produced by training on large teleoperated datasets. These datasets typically consist of free-space trajectories, making them difficult to transfer to test-time environments with obstacles. Previous methods for closing this gap have largely fallen into two groups. Dataset augmentation addresses it at training time but needs obstacle geometry in advance, whereas steering an existing checkpoint at inference time avoids that requirement but is limited in flexibility. Our method draws from both areas without inheriting either drawback. DetAug applies an obstacle-blind augmentation scheme to the transit phases of a free-space dataset, leaving object interactions untouched, and records the augmentation parameters as an explicit conditioning label. At inference it samples a batch of labels and executes the trajectory with the lowest collision cost. On the SafeLIBERO benchmark DetAug achieves a collision-free success rate more than 20pp above the next best method, and selecting over the label space outperforms guidance on the same policy by 26pp. On real hardware, inference-time steering methods collapse on tasks requiring large detours, while DetAug matches or exceeds an obstacle-conditioned baseline without ever seeing obstacles in training.

</details>


### [154] [Hardware-Free Robotics Laboratories in Mixed Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18434)
*Santiago Berrezueta-Guzman,Habiba-Loai Khalil,Andrei Koshelev,Vanesa Metaj,Stefan Wagner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MR-Robotics LAB混合现实平台，将MATLAB生成的机器人轨迹在Meta Quest 3中以真实尺度回放于学习者物理空间，实现无硬件条件下的机器人教学可视化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人教学依赖屏幕仿真，坐标系抽象、非真实尺度，物理硬件受成本、安全与排期限制而难以普及。

Method: 浏览器端服务校验.mat文件、归一化单位并发布带版本的JSON轨迹；Unity在Meta Quest 3上以位置控制复现关节配置，按声明帧率在含碰撞检测与末端抓取的物理场景中回放。单组形成性评价（工程学生）。

Result: 学生报告低配置负担（M=4.67/5）、多视角观察对工作空间理解有帮助（M=4.56），83%愿意在入门机器人课程中使用；仅测量感知结果，无对照与学习成效指标。

Conclusion: 贡献为可复用的"仿真到MR"轨迹通路及无硬件机器人可视化的教育设计指导，不主张相对桌面仿真的比较优势。

Abstract: Teaching robotics relies on screen-based simulation, showing robot motion in an abstract coordinate frame rather than at real scale in the learner's own space, while access to physical hardware is limited by cost, safety, and scheduling constraints. We present MR-Robotics LAB, a mixed-reality (MR) platform that replays MATLAB-generated robot trajectories at real scale within the learner's physical environment. A browser-based service validates a MATLAB workspace file (.mat), normalizes units, and publishes a versioned JSON trajectory; a Unity application on a Meta Quest 3 then reproduces the authored joint configurations under position control and replays them at the declared frame rate within a physics-enabled scene that supports collision detection and end-effector grasping. A formative single-group evaluation with engineering students found that participants reported low setup effort (M = 4.67 on a 5-point scale) and perceived support for workspace understanding from multi-viewpoint inspection (M = 4.56), and 83% of participants affirmed their willingness to use the platform in an introductory robotics course. The evaluation instrument records only perceived outcomes, without counterbalancing or a learning measure, so no comparative advantage over desktop simulation is claimed. The contribution is a reusable simulation-to-MR trajectory pathway and design guidance for hardware-free robot visualization in engineering education.

</details>


### [155] [VLM-MPPI: Grounding Natural Language in Behaviorally Diverse Trajectories for Aerial Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18451)
*Hanbing Zhang,Fangguo Zhao,Zerui Li,Xin Guan,Peng Cheng,Shuo Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分层无人机导航框架：用六个行为条件化的并行MPPI规划器生成多样候选轨迹，将3D轨迹投影到机载FPV图像上，由预训练VLM异步选择候选索引，实现自然语言意图到可行飞行行为的对齐，并在Isaac Sim与真实四旋翼上验证100%任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内杂乱环境中的无人机导航需弥合抽象自然语言语义与低层飞行控制之间的鸿沟；同时视觉语言模型推理延迟高，难以直接用于实时闭环控制。作者希望获得一组有意设计的、语义上可区分的飞行行为，而非随机采样的轨迹变体，从而把语言落地问题转化为可异步执行的视觉动作选择问题。

Method: 并行运行六个"行为条件化"的MPPI规划器，通过设计模式特定的引导代价（guiding cost）与采样偏置，使各模态收敛到不同的行为均值，形成紧凑且有意多样的候选集；将3D候选轨迹投影到机载第一人称RGB流上生成叠加可视化，把语言接地转成视觉动作选择；预训练VLM依据叠加图像与语言提示异步输出候选索引，MPPI以20Hz重规划，PID底层控制器跟踪被选轨迹；整套流水线在NVIDIA Isaac Sim与装配LiDAR、RGB的真实四旋翼上实现。

Result: 仿真与真实飞行实验显示：行为多样性具有语义意义，尽管存在VLM延迟仍能保持稳健的语言对齐，各模式下飞行安全且可重复，在评估场景中达到100%任务成功率。

Conclusion: 将行为条件化MPPI采样与异步VLM动作选择相结合，是在杂乱室内环境中落地自然语言无人机导航的有效方案；"候选轨迹可视化为图像 + VLM选索引"的架构可规避VLM实时性瓶颈，且已具备仿真到真机的可行性验证。

Abstract: We present a hierarchical UAV navigation framework that aligns natural-language intent with dynamically feasible flight behaviors in cluttered indoor environments. To bridge the gap between abstract semantics and low-level control, we employ a parallelized ensemble of six behavior-conditioned Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) planners. Crucially, by designing mode-specific guiding costs and sampling biases, we induce distinct trajectory modes that converge to unique behavioral means, yielding a compact set of intentionally diverse candidates rather than mere stochastic variations. We project these 3D candidates onto the onboard first-person-view RGB stream, turning language grounding into a visual action selection problem. A pretrained vision--language model (VLM) asynchronously selects the candidate index given the overlaid FPV image and a natural-language prompt, while MPPI replans at 20Hz and a PID-based low-level controller tracks the selected trajectory. We implement the full pipeline in NVIDIA Isaac Sim and on a real-world quadrotor platform equipped with LiDAR and RGB sensing. Experiments in both simulation and real-world flights show semantically meaningful behavior diversity, robust language alignment despite VLM latency, and safe, repeatable flight across all modes, achieving 100% task success in our evaluated scenarios.

</details>


### [156] [ForwardDLO: Model-Based Bimanual Shape Matching of Unconstrained Deformable Linear Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18455)
*Tim Missal,Berk Guler,Lucas Domingues,Simon Manschitz,Jan Peters,Paula Dornhofer Paro Costa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ForwardDLO，一种用于非固定双臂绳索形状控制的循环潜动力学模型，逐步预测各线段位移，无显式线段间操作，可批量廉价评估候选动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绳索、线缆等可变形线性物体（DLO）的形状控制在机器人操作中仍是难题。非固定场景下双臂可沿物体任意处抓取，动作空间组合爆炸，且动力学模型单次预测开销限制了规划器可搜索的候选动作数量。

Method: 循环潜动力学模型，以观测到的绳索状态为基，预测每段位移；不含显式 segment-to-segment 运算，使候选动作可批量并行评估；面向非固定双臂场景设计。

Result: 开环预测真实绳索运动误差最低，比最强基线低 13%；固定时间预算内候选动作评估量为同精度模型的 8-22 倍；真实形状匹配相当；30Hz 仿真布线任务成功率 98%，基线在其自身预算下至多 30%。

Conclusion: 高效潜动力学建模可显著提升 DLO 形状控制的规划吞吐与任务成功率，并开源模型、代码及 242 万仿真/14,107 条真实绳索转移数据集。

Abstract: Ropes, cables, and other deformable linear objects appear in tasks from untangling to cable routing and suturing, yet controlling their shape remains a challenge in robot manipulation. We study model-based shape control in a general setting: the object lies unfixated on a support surface and two arms may grasp and move it anywhere along its length. Because each arm chooses a grasp point, direction, and magnitude, the joint action space is combinatorially large, and the dynamics model's per-prediction cost bounds how much of it a planner can search. We present ForwardDLO, a recurrent latent dynamics model for this unfixated bimanual setting that predicts per-segment displacements grounded in the observed rope state at every step. Our model reaches accuracy comparable to more expensive baselines while containing no explicit segment-to-segment operations, which makes batched evaluation of candidate actions cheap. On open-loop prediction of real rope motion it reaches the lowest error of the learned models we evaluate, 13% below the strongest baseline. Within a fixed time budget it scores 8 to 22 times more candidate actions than models of comparable accuracy while matching them in real-world shape matching; and on a simulated routing task at a 30Hz control rate, this throughput converts into 98% task success versus at most 30% for the baselines at their own budgets. We release the model, code, and a dataset of 2.42 million simulated and 14,107 real rope transitions at this https URL

</details>


### [157] [Real-Time Bounded Catenary Solver for UAV Tether Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18482)
*Max Beffert,Andreas Zell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: For non-stationary tethered multirotor UAVs in real-world conditions, simulating the forces imposed on the drone by the aerodynamic drag of the tether becomes crucial, with online use cases placing a hard bound on the maximum solve time. In previous work, a quasi-analytical catenary tether model reached a mean solve time of 0.51 ms using a general-purpose root finder, but without any worst-case guarantees or proven convergence. In this work, we reformulate the inner solver by reducing the catenary boundary-value problem to a single transcendental equation in one well-conditioned unknown. We derive a closed-form bracket and prove monotonicity and convexity as well as existence and uniqueness of the root, which together guarantee convergence of the solver. We further propose a two-regime initial guess which approximates the true root within 3.4% and reduces the mean iteration count by 68.0% to 2.36 compared to the textbook initialization. Building on the hybrid root-finding method rtsafe (Newton-Raphson with bisection fallback giving bounded iteration counts), we implement a specialized variant that exploits the problem structure to omit unnecessary checks while retaining correctness, which gives up to 1.3 times speedup. With the proposed solver the full tether model achieves a nearly constant solve time of 6.9 us on average and 7.7 us at worst, a 40 times speedup over an optimized re-implementation of the previous method, while agreeing with it to a relative deviation of 8.7e-9. Because the reformulation leaves the underlying physical model untouched, the experimental validation of the previous work carries over unchanged. We further demonstrate its suitability for embedded, resource-constrained platforms with a Lua implementation running directly in ArduPilot on a drone's flight controller, where it stays well inside the scheduling budget with a mean solve time of 0.74 ms.

</details>


### [158] [ActionPiece: Rethinking Action Tokenization for Autoregressive Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18487)
*Shijie Lian,Bin Yu,Zhaolong Shen,Xiaopeng Lin,Yichao Du,Zhirui Zhang,Laurence T. Yang,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出物理秩一致性（PRC）指标衡量动作分词对局部物理距离排序的保真度，并给出ActionPiece：通过表征学习与量化的联合监督（物理秩保持+量化正则）生成离散动作token。同一Qwen3-VL-4B策略训练设定下，LIBERO 94.8%、LIBERO-Plus 68.8%、SimplerEnv 71.9%、VLA-Arena L0-L2 51.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归VLA模型中，动作分词器既决定策略训练目标，也决定预测token解码出的可执行指令。现有保真度评估多用MSE等逐点重构指标，但逐点误差小并不意味着动作的相对调整（跨示范的局部差异）被保留：压缩后相似动作可能聚集在代表性运动附近，而不同情境所需的微调被削弱、扭曲甚至反向。因此需要一种衡量关系保真度的指标。

Method: 1) 提出PRC（物理秩一致性）：在解码动作上度量重构后局部物理距离排序的保持程度，可跨词表与解码器架构对比，作为逐点指标的补充；2) 提出ActionPiece，联合监督表征学习与量化：物理秩保持目标监督编码器特征距离与量化特征距离中的远近排序，量化正则将该排序施加到码字分配分布上；3) 两个目标与重构损失联合，生成离散动作token，供标准自回归策略学习，并通过冻结解码器执行。

Result: 在相同Qwen3-VL-4B策略训练设置下，ActionPiece在LIBERO达94.8%，未见过的LIBERO-Plus达68.8%，SimperEnv 71.9%，VLA-Arena L0-L2平均51.5%。组件消融显示两个目标共同提升PRC与策略成功率。

Conclusion: 以PRC补充逐点重构指标，用物理关系监督（秩保持+量化正则）改进动作分词，能在不改变策略学习与执行框架的前提下提升VLA的泛化成功率，说明物理关系监督对动作分词具有实用价值。但证据以仿真基准为主，缺少真实机器人验证。

Abstract: Action tokenizers play a central role in autoregressive vision-language-action (VLA) models, determining both the targets for policy training and the executable commands recovered from predicted tokens. Their fidelity is commonly evaluated using pointwise reconstruction metrics such as mean squared error (MSE), yet small individual errors do not fully characterize how faithfully action adjustments across demonstrations are preserved. After compression, similar actions may still cluster around a representative motion, while the adjustments needed for different contexts are diminished, distorted, or even reversed. We introduce physical rank consistency (PRC) to measure how well tokenization preserves local physical distance rankings after reconstruction. Evaluating decoded actions provides a common reference across token vocabularies and decoder architectures, complementing pointwise accuracy with a measure of relational fidelity. We further present ActionPiece, which preserves physical action relationships through joint supervision of representation learning and quantization. Physical rank preservation supervises near-far ordering in encoder and quantized feature distances, while quantization regularization applies the same ordering to codeword assignment distributions. Both objectives augment reconstruction, producing discrete action tokens for standard autoregressive policy learning and execution through a frozen decoder. Under the same Qwen3-VL-4B policy training setup, ActionPiece achieves 94.8% on LIBERO and 68.8% on unseen LIBERO-Plus, with additional evaluations reaching 71.9% on SimplerEnv and 51.5% across VLA-Arena L0-L2. Component ablations show that the two objectives jointly improve PRC and policy success, demonstrating the value of physical relationship supervision for action tokenization.

</details>


### [159] [TAO-Force: Unifying Force-Aware Perception and Fast-Slow Control for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18497)
*Bohan Gan,Xuanzhang Wen,Yongsheng Zhao,Baoping Cheng,Wenhe Jia,Ye Wang,Gongxin Yao,Han Gao,Jingyao Tang,Lei Zhao,Ji Ge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TAO-Force：一种力条件化的 VLA 框架，通过 F-FiLM 将力反馈注入冻结的视觉-语言主干，并采用接触门控的快慢架构（慢速位置控制 + 快速导纳控制）实现对接触丰富操作任务的顺应执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型以视觉为主、采用位置控制，难以感知接触起始与相互作用强度，也无法对快速变化的接触动力学做出顺应响应，因此在接触丰富操作任务上表现不足。

Method: 提出 TAO-Force：(1) 感知层面引入 Force-conditioned Feature-wise Linear Modulation（F-FiLM），将编码后的力反馈注入冻结预训练视觉-语言主干的表示中，保留语义先验；(2) 控制层面采用接触门控快慢架构，非接触阶段由慢速位置控制分支跟踪标称轨迹，接触阶段由快速导纳控制分支调节物理交互。

Result: 在力感知任务上进行了详细分析，并在四个真实世界接触丰富操作任务上完成评估，验证了方法的有效性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 力感知与接触调节执行相结合的力条件化 VLA 框架可有效弥补纯视觉感知与位置控制在接触丰富操作中的双重缺口。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong performance across diverse robotic manipulation tasks, yet their predominantly vision-centric perception and position-controlled execution remain insufficient for contact-rich manipulation. Visual observations alone often provide limited evidence of contact onset and interaction magnitude, while position-control policies cannot respond compliantly to rapidly changing contact dynamics. To bridge both the perception and control gaps, we propose TAO-Force, a force-conditioned VLA framework that combines force-aware policy learning with contact-regulated execution. For force-aware perception, TAO-Force introduces Force-conditioned Feature-wise Linear Modulation (F-FiLM) to inject encoded force feedback into the representations of a frozen pretrained visual-language backbone while preserving its semantic priors. For responsive control, it employs a contact-gated fast-slow architecture, with a slow position-control branch tracking nominal trajectories during non-contact phases and a fast admittance-control branch regulating physical interaction during contact phases. Detailed analyses on a force-perception task and real-world evaluations across four contact-rich manipulation tasks validate the effectiveness and robustness of TAO-Force.

</details>


### [160] [InterMASH: A Unified Geometric Representation for Grasp Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18504)
*Xuanze Yang,Yumeng Liu,Haiyang Xin,Changhao Li,Haowei Shen,Kai Xu,Ligang Liu,Ruizhen Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 InterMASH，一种基于球面固定锚点的统一几何表征，用低阶球谐编码手部几何、物体几何与接触，形成显式可解释的 token 序列，并用条件 Diffusion Transformer 在该表征空间直接联合生成手部几何与接触，实现人手与机器人手的跨本体对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抓取合成是人手建模与机器人操作的基础问题，但人手与机器人手因形态和表面建模差异，缺乏统一表征。已有方法依赖接触图或稠密隐式描述子，前者信息不完整，后者计算昂贵且冗余。

Method: 1) 用球面固定锚点建立跨本体对应；2) 每个锚点用低阶球谐紧凑编码局部手部几何、物体几何与接触，构成显式、可解释的 token 序列；3) 在该原生 token 化表征上构建条件 Diffusion Transformer，直接在 InterMASH 空间中联合生成手部几何与接触。

Result: 在大规模 ShadowHand 基准上，关键物理可行性指标达到与 SOTA 竞争的水平；支持多手联合训练；实验表明用人手抓取数据做跨本体微调可提升机器人抓取成功率与多样性。

Conclusion: InterMASH 提供了一种紧凑、显式、可解释的跨本体统一抓取表征，并验证了人手数据向机器人手迁移的有效性，为统一的人手—机器人手抓取建模提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Grasp synthesis aims to generate stable and physically plausible hand--object interactions, and has become a fundamental problem in both human hand modeling and robotic manipulation. However, a unified representation across human and robotic hands is still lacking, mainly due to differences in hand morphology and surface modeling. Prior methods typically rely on either contact maps or dense implicit descriptors to represent interaction, but these representations are often incomplete or computationally expensive and redundant. We propose InterMASH, a unified geometric representation that establishes cross-embodiment correspondence using sphere-fixed anchors. At each anchor, low-degree spherical harmonics compactly encode local hand geometry, object geometry, and contact, forming an explicit and interpretable token sequence. Building on this natively tokenized structure, we introduce a conditional Diffusion Transformer that operates directly in the proposed InterMASH representation space and jointly generates hand geometry and contact, improving consistency and physical plausibility. Our method achieves competitive performance with state-of-the-art methods on key physical feasibility metrics in a large-scale ShadowHand benchmark, supports joint training across multiple hands, and shows that cross-embodiment fine-tuning with human grasp data can improve robotic grasp success and diversity. Project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [161] [ActiveScale: Scaling Active Perception for Robots across Model, Data, and Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18514)
*Shuai Zhou,Kaisheng Pang,Wenxuan Song,Wenjie Zhang,Xinhu Zheng,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ActiveScale 通过模型、数据与硬件协同设计推进机器人主动感知：为 VLA 增加历史视频观测与显式相机位姿监督（逐帧 pose token + 轻量预测头），并大规模利用第一视角人类数据做中训练，同时提出单操作者遥操作移动操作平台 AMP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定视角下任务相关信息常被遮挡或不可观测，主动感知对机器人操作至关重要；但现有 VLA 难以在不同视角间推理并主动获取有效观测，缺乏跨视角关联、位姿监督与可扩展数据。

Method: （1）模型：在 VLA 中引入历史视频观测与逐帧相机位姿 token 及轻量预测头，实现跨视角观测关联与场景一致理解；（2）数据：利用人类活动中的自然相机运动，构建 1000 小时第一人称与机器人数据的人-机中训练配方，适配时序输入与位姿监督；（3）硬件：Active-perception Mobile-manipulation Platform（AMP），支持单操作者遥操作下的主动感知与移动操作，可规模化采集视角变化与操作协同的演示。

Result: 在主动感知任务上成功率提升；消融实验验证了相机位姿感知建模与第一人称中训练的贡献。

Conclusion: 模型、数据、硬件三方面的协同组件为机器人操作中的主动感知研究与开发提供了集成化基础。

Abstract: Active perception is essential for robotic manipulation when fixed viewpoints leave task-relevant information occluded or unobserved. However, enabling vision-language-action (VLA) models to reason across changing viewpoints and actively acquire informative observations remains challenging. We present ActiveScale, a framework that advances active perception through coordinated model, data, and hardware designs. Our model augments a VLA with historical video observations and explicit camera-pose supervision, using per-frame pose tokens and a lightweight prediction head to associate observations across viewpoints and support a coherent understanding of the scene. To learn from the camera motion naturally present in human activity, we introduce a scalable human--robot mid-training recipe using 1000 hours of egocentric and robotic data, adapting the model to temporal inputs and pose supervision. We further introduce Active-perception Mobile-manipulation Platform (AMP), a robotic platform that supports active perception and mobile manipulation through single-operator teleoperation, enabling scalable collection of demonstrations that coordinate viewpoint changes and manipulation. Experiments demonstrate improved success rates on active-perception tasks, while ablation studies validate the contributions of camera-pose-aware modeling and egocentric mid-training. Together, these components provide an integrated foundation for studying and developing active perception in robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [162] [DynoFluxBench: Benchmarking Kinodynamic Space-Time Planners in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18549)
*Franz Queißner,Andreas Orthey,Wolfgang Hönig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出动态环境下运动动力学规划基准框架DynoFluxBench，并开发ST-Db-RRT、ST-GBRRT、KIST三种融合运动动力学与时空搜索的规划器，建立强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在非结构化、动态环境中运动需同时满足运动动力学可行性与避障安全，但缺乏同时涵盖这两方面的专用基准框架。

Method: 构建DynoFluxBench，用于在已知动态环境、到达时间无界条件下比较运动动力学规划器；提出三种融合运动动力学与时空方法的规划器：ST-Db-RRT基于轨迹优化扩展随机选择的间断有界运动基元，KIST与ST-GBRRT以不同启发式引导维护运动动力学可行树；并分析其概率完备性保证。

Result: 在DynoFluxBench上评估表明，ST-Db-RRT获得首个解的速度最高快32倍，而KIST与ST-GBRRT在轨迹优化易失效的场景下仍具价值。

Conclusion: DynoFluxBench为动态环境运动动力学规划提供了可比较的基准与强基线，三类规划器呈现速度与鲁棒性的互补权衡。

Abstract: Robots that leave structured, static environments must plan motions that are kinodynamically feasible and safe among moving obstacles. However, there are no dedicated benchmark frameworks that combine both aspects. To overcome this, we present DynoFluxBench, a framework to compare kinodynamic planners in known, dynamic environments with unbounded arrival time. To demonstrate its utility and establish strong baselines, we develop three dedicated planners, named ST-Db-RRT, ST-GBRRT, and KIST, that fuse kinodynamic and space-time methods, covering different kinodynamic search paradigms: ST-Db-RRT expands with randomly selected discontinuity-bounded motion primitives using trajectory optimization, whereas KIST and ST-GBRRT maintain a kinodynamically feasible tree with different heuristic guidance. We analyze the probabilistic completeness guarantees of those new planners in dynamic environments. Finally, we evaluate ST-Db-RRT, ST-GBRRT, and KIST using DynoFluxBench, showing that ST-Db-RRT reaches a first solution up to 32 times faster, while KIST and ST-GBRRT remain valuable where trajectory optimization is fragile. Videos and further analysis can be found at this https URL .

</details>


### [163] [GroundingVLN: Reasoning and Acting with Grounding for Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18581)
*Kailing Li,Yu Han,Tianwen Qian,Yuqian Fu,Jingyu Gong,Jiangming Shi,Xiaoling Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 GroundingVLN，用视觉 grounding 作为推理与动作间的共享接口：推理时把任务相关视觉证据锚定到图像位置，执行时预测进度对齐的像素目标交由几何规划器转成动作；并构建 GroundingCOTVLN-188K 数据集与 GEAR 强化学习。R2R-CE 69.9% SR、RxR-CE 75.1% SR，仅用最强基线 0.9% 训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 驱动的 VLN agent 存在两个耦合缺口：中间推理未显式锚定到视觉证据，高层决策缺乏精确空间目标来引导低层运动，导致语义推理与空间执行脱节。认知科学表明人类导航通过锚定地标、导向空间目标的层级方式弥合该鸿沟。

Method: 以视觉 grounding 作为推理与动作的统一接口：1) 推理即 grounding——在结构化推理链中将任务相关视觉证据锚定到精确图像位置（grounded CoT）；2) 行动经由 grounding——预测进度对齐的像素级目标，由几何规划器转换为底层动作。训练上构建时间对齐的 grounded 推理轨迹数据集 GroundingCOTVLN-188K，并提出 GEAR（Grounded and Execution-Aware RL），将 grounded 推理与空间决策对齐到下游执行。

Result: R2R-CE 达 69.9% SR，RxR-CE 达 75.1% SR，均为 SOTA；样本效率高，训练数据量仅为最强基线的 0.9%。跨数据集泛化强：仅在 R2R 训练时 RxR-CE 达 59.9% SR，比最强基线提升 20.1%。

Conclusion: grounding 可作为连接语义推理与空间执行的共享接口，配合 grounded CoT 数据与执行感知 RL，能在极少数据下显著提升 VLN 性能与泛化性。

Abstract: Although vision-language models (VLMs) possess strong visual understanding and reasoning capabilities, existing vision-and-language navigation (VLN) agents struggle to connect semantic reasoning with spatial execution. Two coupled gaps remain in this connection, as intermediate reasoning is not explicitly anchored to visual evidence and high-level decisions lack precise spatial goals to guide low-level motion. Cognitive science suggests that human navigation bridges these levels hierarchically by anchoring cognition to relevant landmarks and guiding locomotion toward spatial goals. Motivated by this principle, we propose GroundingVLN, which uses visual grounding as a shared interface between reasoning and action. GroundingVLN first reasons with grounding by anchoring task-relevant visual evidence to precise image locations throughout structured reasoning. It then acts through grounding by predicting a progress-aligned pixel goal that a geometric planner translates into primitive actions. To learn these capabilities, we construct GroundingCOTVLN-188K, a dataset of temporally aligned grounded reasoning traces, and introduce Grounded and Execution-Aware Reinforcement Learning (GEAR), which aligns grounded reasoning and spatial decisions with downstream execution. Experiments demonstrate that GroundingVLN achieves state-of-the-art performance (69.9% SR on R2R-CE and 75.1% SR on RxR-CE) with high sample efficiency, using just 0.9% as much training data as the strongest baseline. It also generalizes strongly across datasets, attaining 59.9% SR on RxR-CE when trained solely on R2R, a gain of 20.1% over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [164] [DeformSmith: Physics Harness-Guided Hierarchical Generation of Deformable Assets for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18620)
*Can Li,Jie Gu,Zishun Deng,Jingmin Chen,Lei Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DeformSmith：从文本或单图自动生成可交互、物理可信的可形变资产，通过分层智能体构建与物理测试闭环，结合机器人操作反馈迭代优化几何、物理模型与材质行为，视觉与物理合理性均优于PhysGen3D/PhysGM/PhysX-Omni等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可形变操作资产的生成需同时确定几何、外观与物理属性，而文本与图像对形变与接触响应提供的证据十分有限，却直接决定其可操作性。因此需要自动化方法在生成中解决耦合的物理约束，并利用交互证据指导构建与精修。

Method: 提出DeformSmith框架：基于分层智能体式构建流程（hierarchical agentic construction）与共享的物理接地测试环境（shared physics-grounded harness），逐步构建、测试并精修几何、物理模型、材质行为与机器人交互；以机器人操作的操控反馈与可回放交互数据闭合生成循环。

Result: 在视觉质量与物理合理性上优于PhysGen3D、PhysGM、PhysX-Omni等SOTA基线，并支持为可形变物体机器人操作合成数据。

Conclusion: 交互证据驱动的智能体闭环生成可作为可形变可操作资产自动化构建的有效范式，兼顾视觉与物理可信度并支撑操作数据合成。

Abstract: Creating deformable assets for robot manipulation requires jointly specifying their geometry, appearance, and physical properties. This is especially challenging for deformable objects, since text and images provide limited evidence about how they deform and respond to contact, yet these responses directly affect their suitability for interaction. Automated generation therefore needs to resolve coupled physical requirements and use interaction evidence to guide construction and refinement. We present DeformSmith, a framework that enables automated generation of interactive, physically credible deformable assets from text or a single image. Through hierarchical agentic construction and a shared physics-grounded harness, it progressively builds, tests, and refines geometry, physical models, material behavior, and robot interaction until the resulting asset is ready for simulation and manipulation. Robot interaction closes the generation loop through manipulation feedback and replayable interaction data. Results show that DeformSmith generates assets with better visual quality and physical plausibility than state-of-the-art baselines, including PhysGen3D, PhysGM, and PhysX-Omni, while supporting the synthesis of data for robotic manipulation of deformable objects. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [165] [Benchmarking Visual-Inertial Odometry in Subterranean Environments Under Sensor Degradation, Miscalibration, and Dynamic Occlusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18628)
*Yueying Zhu,Xiang Li,Thien-Minh Nguyen,Xuehe Wang,Shenghai Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于CERBERUS数据集提出VIO失效导向压力测试基准，在9类扰动下评测滤波/优化/学习三类共4个VIO系统，用覆盖率和失效阈值揭示鲁棒性边界与部署脆弱性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地下无GPS环境下VIO是自主作业核心能力，但传感器漂移、标定误差与动态遮挡会显著降低其可靠性；现有评测多只关注标称工况精度，无法说明实际部署中何时会失效，缺少面向失效的鲁棒性评估视角。

Method: 构建失效导向的VIO压力测试基准，采用地下环境CERBERUS数据集；选取覆盖滤波、优化与学习三类范式的4个代表性VIO系统；设计9种贴近实际的扰动设置，包括IMU零偏与噪声变化、相机内参和外参漂移、动态场景遮挡；除轨迹误差外，引入覆盖率与失效阈值等指标刻画鲁棒性极限。

Result: 揭示标称精度无法体现的失效行为；不同VIO范式呈现明显不同的脆弱性模式——部分方法对惯性退化更敏感，另一些更受几何标定误差或动态干扰影响。

Conclusion: 为挑战性地下场景中的VIO选型、标定优先级排序和可靠运行提供面向部署的指导；将开源完整基准脚本与评测流水线以支持可复现评测和后续扩展。

Abstract: Visual-inertial odometry (VIO) is a core capability for autonomous operation in GPS-denied subterranean environments, yet its reliability can degrade sharply under sensor drift, calibration errors, and dynamic occlusion. Existing evaluations mainly emphasize nominal-condition accuracy, offering limited insight into when practical deployment failures occur. In this work, we present a failure-centric stress-test benchmark for VIO in underground environments using the CERBERUS dataset. We systematically evaluate four representative VIO systems spanning filtering-, optimization-, and learning-based paradigms under nine practical perturbation settings, including IMU bias and noise variation, camera intrinsic and extrinsic drift, and dynamic scene occlusion. Beyond conventional trajectory error, we analyze robustness limits through coverage ratio and failure thresholds, revealing breakdown behaviors that are not captured by nominal-condition performance alone. Our study shows distinct vulnerability patterns across VIO paradigms: some methods are more sensitive to inertial degradation, while others are more affected by geometric miscalibration or dynamic interference. These results provide deployment-oriented guidance for VIO selection, calibration prioritization, and reliable operation in challenging underground scenarios. To support reproducible evaluation and future extensions, we will release the full benchmark scripts and evaluation pipeline.

</details>


### [166] [From Gameplay to Policy: Towards Scalable Robot Data Collection via Gamified Robot-Free Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18650)
*Zheng Li,Liang Zhu,Junzhe Wang,Huayuan Chen,Ziyun Liu,Jiahang Cao,Xinyu Sheng,Pei Qu,Yufei Jia,Ximeng Zhang,Jiarui Xie,Zizhao Yuan,Haoang Li,Yi Cai,Jinni Zhou,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VR游戏化第一人称数据采集平台Project Kitchen与Game2Policy方法，从游戏轨迹提取具身无关的affordance线索（接触点、子目标状态），预训练后用少量真机示教微调，仿真提升10.0点、真机提升18.3点成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人操作数据采集成本高、难规模化；现有方案或绑定特定硬件限制众包与迁移，或标注不完整、行为多样性不足。受游戏长期吸引玩家参与的启发，作者希望把数据采集转化为游戏化体验，并将人类操作经验迁移到真实机器人。

Method: 构建Project Kitchen：基于VR的游戏化第一人称数据采集平台，不依赖特定机器人本体与硬件，可大规模部署。为弥合game-to-real差距，提出Game2Policy：从游戏轨迹中提取具身不变的affordance线索（接触点、子目标状态），在游戏数据上预训练affordance模型，再仅用少量真机示教与下游策略联合微调。

Result: few-shot设定下，Game2Policy在仿真中平均成功率提升10.0点，在真实机器人上提升18.3点。用户研究与定量分析显示Project Kitchen能激发多样化的操作行为并提供有吸引力的采集体验。

Conclusion: 游戏化虚拟环境可作为可扩展的操作知识来源，为通用可泛化操作策略的数据采集提供新范式；平台与代码将在接收后开源。

Abstract: Learning generalizable robot manipulation policies requires large-scale and diverse interaction data, yet collecting real-world demonstrations remains costly and difficult to scale. Existing approaches to data collection are either dependent on specific robot hardware that limits crowdsourcing and transferability, or suffer from incomplete annotation and limited behavioral diversity. Inspired by how games sustain long-term human engagement, we explore an alternative paradigm that turns data collection into an engaging gameplay experience and transfers the resulting human manipulation experience to real robots. We present Project Kitchen, a VR-based gamified egocentric data collection platform that elicits diverse, goal-directed manipulation while remaining independent of specific robot embodiments and hardware, making it applicable to broader and potentially large-scale deployment. To bridge the game-to-real gap, we further introduce Game2Policy, which extracts embodiment-invariant affordance cues, including contact points and sub-goal states, from gameplay trajectories. An affordance model is pre-trained on game-collected data and then jointly fine-tuned with downstream policies using only a handful of real-robot demonstrations. Experiments show that Game2Policy improves average success rates by 10.0 points in simulation and 18.3 points on real robots in the few-shot setting. User studies and quantitative analyses further show that Project Kitchen promotes diverse manipulation behaviors and provides an engaging data collection experience. These results demonstrate the potential of gamified virtual environments as a scalable source of manipulation knowledge. The platform and code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [167] [FIERCE: From Generalist Robot Policies to Fast Specialists via Progress-Failure Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18651)
*Runjia Tan,Yuang Tu,Yujie Yan,Lan Yu,Xuesong Tian,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FIERCE 提出一种通用策略初始化的强化学习框架，核心是统一的任务自适应进度-失败评估器，实现无需持续通用动作查询、目标域模拟器或人工稠密奖励的紧凑专家微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略可提供良好初始化，但将其精炼为紧凑专家时，受限于物理交互样本量少，需要信息量高的学习反馈；现有方法依赖稠密奖励标注、目标任务模拟器或持续调用通用策略，成本高。

Method: 评估器在观测-语言表示上共享两个头：观测进度头与动作条件隐式预测器，后者用过去与当前预测输入因果序列头估计任务失败；用进度标签与偏好标签、命令-观测同步、终止结果联合监督训练，目标域 rollout 用于适配与校准。固定的评估器快照提供进度塑形与失败风险惩罚，终端奖励独立校验；评估器与策略交替更新。

Result: 评测从反馈质量、策略学习效率与部署成本三方面，在仿真与两个接触丰富的真实任务上展开；部署时仅保留紧凑专家。代码、模型权重与数据还原工具已开源。

Conclusion: FIERCE 用统一的进度-失败评估器替代稠密奖励与模拟器依赖，使通用策略到紧凑专家的精炼更高效，并提供开源资源，为真机策略精炼提供可复用的通用初始化-强化学习范式。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies offer useful initialization, but refining compact specialists through limited physical interaction requires informative learning feedback. We present FIERCE, a generalist-initialized reinforcement learning framework centered on a unified, task-adaptive progress-failure evaluator. Its architecture shares an observation-language representation between an observed-progress head and an action-conditioned latent predictor whose past and current predictions feed a causal sequence head for task-failure estimation. Joint supervision from progress and preference labels, synchronized commands and observations, and terminal outcomes trains the evaluator; target-task rollouts support adaptation and calibration. Fixed evaluator snapshots provide progress shaping and failure-risk penalties alongside independently verified terminal rewards, while evaluator and policy updates alternate as new experience is collected. Refinement requires neither continued generalist action queries nor a dedicated target-task simulator or manually annotated dense rewards. Only the compact specialist is retained at deployment. The evaluation separates feedback quality, policy-learning efficiency, and deployment cost across simulation and two contact-rich real tasks. Code, model weights, and data-restoration tools are released at this https URL .

</details>


### [168] [VLA-ULAP: Interleaving Cloud VLA Calls with Ultra-Lightweight Local Action Prediction at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18663)
*Deyu Cao,Ryuji Oi,Kosuke Matsushima,Yuxuan Pan,Ziheng Wang,Daichi Fujiki,Atsutake Kosuge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA-ULAP 用约 7.4M 参数的轻量本地动作预测器在远程 VLA 调用之间做插值预测，减少 48.8–76.7% 的 VLA 调用并保留 95–97.5% 成功率，同时大幅降低推理时延与能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 十亿参数级 VLA 策略需要大量机载算力，而远程推理的通信延迟又会阻碍及时响应。作者希望在几乎不损失成功率的前提下，降低对远程大模型 VLA 的调用频率，实现快速、低功耗的本地动作生成。

Method: 提出 VLA-ULAP：将远程 VLA 调用与超轻量本地动作预测器 ULAP 交错执行。ULAP 含冻结视觉编码器共约 7.4M 参数，融合当前视图、本体感知与已执行动作历史，单次前向预测动作块；独立训练，不需要 VLA 隐状态、在线验证或服务器往返。

Result: Jetson Orin Nano 上 ULAP 单次推理 19.9 ms、0.183 J，而 RTX A6000 上 GR00T 为 284.3 ms、50.55 J。三个仿真基座策略/基准组合中，选定工作点去除 48.8–76.7% 的 VLA 调用，保留 95.0–97.5% 基线成功率。VLA-JEPA 上相比 ACT 省约 49.2% 推理时间、51.0% GPU 能耗（等成功率下比 SP-VLA 省 77.1% 时间、79.9% 能耗）。SO-101 真机实验在已见与未见位置保留 95.2–100% 成功率，推理时间降约 47.9–58.0%、推理设备能耗降 52.1–62.5%。在 LIBERO-Safety 延迟感知仿真中两个任务分别超过 π0.5 达 11.0 与 15.5 个百分点，同时 VLA 调用约减半。

Conclusion: 将远程大 VLA 与极轻量本地动作预测器交错，可在保持成功率的同时显著降低时延与能耗，并改善延迟敏感任务表现；方法独立训练、无需在线验证，便于部署到边缘设备。

Abstract: Billion-parameter vision--language--action (VLA) policies demand substantial onboard power, while communication delays in remote inference hinder timely responses. We propose VLA-ULAP, which interleaves remote VLA calls with an Ultra-Lightweight Local Action Predictor (ULAP). With approximately 7.4M parameters including the frozen vision encoder, ULAP combines current views, proprioception, and executed action history to predict chunks in one pass. Trained independently, it requires no VLA hidden states, online verification, or server round trips. On Jetson Orin Nano, ULAP takes 19.9 ms and 0.183 J per inference, compared with 284.3 ms and 50.55 J for GR00T on RTX A6000. Across three simulated base-policy/benchmark pairs, selected operating points remove 48.8--76.7\% of VLA calls while retaining 95.0--97.5\% of the baseline success rate. Against local VLA-acceleration alternatives on VLA-JEPA, ULAP uses an estimated 49.2\% less inference time and 51.0\% less GPU energy per successful episode than ACT at comparable success rates, and 77.1\% less time and 79.9\% less energy than SP-VLA at equal success rates. Physical SO-101 experiments retain 95.2--100\% of the baseline success rate across seen and held-out placements while reducing inference time by an estimated 47.9--58.0\% and inference-device energy by 52.1--62.5\%, based on successful-episode call counts and measured device costs. Faster responses also improve dynamic-task success rates: in latency-aware LIBERO-Safety simulation, VLA-ULAP exceeds $\pi_{0.5}$ by 11.0 and 15.5 percentage points on two tasks while approximately halving VLA calls.

</details>


### [169] [M$^3$P-R1: Reinforcement Learning for Large Language Model Guided Multi-Modal Motion Planning via MIP Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18669)
*Xingpeng Sun,Zherong Pan,Kai Cheng,Xindi Tang,Syed Talha Bukhari,Aniket Bera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 M³P-R1，用强化学习微调 LLM，使其将多模态运动规划任务分解为 MIP 变量、约束与目标，并生成调用 MIP 求解库的可执行 Python 代码，实现求解器支持的稳健可验证规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态运动规划需联合推理连续运动与离散模态切换（如双足先行走再抓取），纯离散或纯连续规划器均难以处理。混合整数规划（MIP）虽提供原则性框架，但在非凸机器人任务所需的近似离散化 MIP 范式下，构建可解公式通常依赖人工与领域知识，难以规模化。

Method: M³P-R1 以强化学习微调大语言模型，将 M³P 任务分解为 MIP 变量、约束和目标；不让模型直接输出答案（易幻觉），而是生成使用 MIP 优化库与约束接口的可执行 Python 代码；以求解器结果为结果驱动奖励进行训练，学习组合模态级离散化原语并合成跨模态耦合约束。

Result: 摘要未给出量化实验结果，仅声明方法可针对复杂 M³P 任务合成可执行 MIP 程序，并借助求解器实现稳健、可验证的求解，规避 LLM 直接输出的幻觉问题。

Conclusion: LLM 结合 RL 与求解器在环的代码生成，为多模态运动规划的 MIP 建模自动化提供了一条可行路径，把领域特定的公式构建转化为可学习、可验证的程序合成问题。

Abstract: Multi-Modal Motion Planning (M$^3$P) requires joint reasoning over continuous motions and discrete mode transitions, making it difficult to solve efficiently. For instance, a bipedal robot may walk to a target location and then use its arms to grasp an object. This scenario captures both mode transitions and continuous dynamics, yielding feasible paths that neither purely discrete nor continuous planners can handle. While Mixed-Integer Programming (MIP) offers a principled framework, constructing tractable formulations for non-convex problems is typically manual and domain-specific, especially in the approximate, discretization-based MIP regime needed for non-convex robotic tasks. We propose M$^3$P-R1, a reinforcement learning method that fine-tunes large language models (LLMs) to decompose M$^3$P tasks into MIP variables, constraints, and objectives. Instead of directly outputting answers, which are often prone to hallucination, the model generates executable Python code using MIP optimization libraries and constraint interfaces. This enables solver-backed execution for robust and verifiable solutions. Trained with an outcome-driven reward against the solver, M$^3$P-R1 learns to compose modality-level discretization primitives and synthesize cross-modal coupling constraints, producing executable MIP programs for complex M$^3$P tasks.

</details>


### [170] [WeaveRL: Weaving Reconstruction into Scene-Aware Fabrics for Perceptive Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18685)
*Remo Steiner,Vikram Ramasamy,David Tingdahl,Sam Mady,Karl Van Wyk,Nathan Ratliff,David Recasens Lafuente,Soha Pouya,Tuur Stuyck,Alex Millane*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GPU加速的surfel重建方法，在数千个并行仿真中实时重建场景几何，使RL策略基于传感器几何而非手工指定几何学习，提升碰撞密集操作任务性能与sim-to-real迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人强化学习难以生成几何复杂操作策略；已有基于几何fabric的碰撞规避控制依赖静态、手工指定的场景表示，缺乏在线主动3D感知，且无法融入大规模并行RL训练。

Method: GPU加速的重建系统，在主动rollout过程中对数千个并行仿真实例以surfel集合形式重建场景；策略在传感器导出的几何（surfel fabrics）上训练，而非手工几何。

Result: 在碰撞密集操作任务上，surfel fabrics使策略能处理原语基线失败的高几何复杂度场景，并保持sim-to-real迁移；场景感知fabric训练的策略对测试时新几何更鲁棒，未见障碍下无碰撞完成率从35%提升至61%。

Conclusion: 将主动在线3D感知与大规模并行RL训练结合可行，提升复杂操作策略的几何泛化与迁移；开源重建系统、训练代码与测试数据集。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning allows robots to acquire complex skills, but producing policies for geometrically complex manipulation remains difficult. A promising approach is to learn on top of collision-avoidant controllers, such as geometric fabrics. However, these approaches have relied on static, hand-specified representations of the scene. Integrating active, online 3D perception into massively parallel RL training has so far been inaccessible. We introduce a GPU-accelerated method that reconstructs the scene as a collection of surfels across thousands of parallel simulation instances during active rollouts. This lets policies operate over sensor-derived, rather than hand-specified, geometry. On a suite of collision-dense manipulation tasks, our surfel fabrics enable policies to tackle geometrically complex scenes where primitive-based baselines fail, while maintaining sim-to-real transfer. Furthermore, policies learned with a scene-aware fabric are more robust to the introduction of novel geometry at test time, improving collision-free task completion under unseen obstacles from 35% to 61%. We release our reconstruction system, training code and test dataset to spur research in this direction.

</details>


### [171] [Toward 3D Printable Non-Planar Electroadhesive Structures for Active Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18700)
*Mostafa A. Atalla,Carol R. Derla,Pep Canyelles Pericàs,Gijs Krijnen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 利用多材料3D打印将导电叉指电极直接集成到平面与圆柱形结构中，实现可电压调控的静电粘附垫。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统静电粘附垫多为平面结构，难以直接贴合非平面表面；作者希望借助多材料3D打印把电极图案嵌入结构本身，从而将静电粘附能力扩展到非平面几何。

Method: 采用多材料3D打印制备静电粘附垫：平面垫作为基准，圆柱形垫作为非平面演示，导电叉指电极作为结构的一部分被打印成型。通过正压力测量评估两种几何下电压对粘附力的调制效果。

Result: 平面与圆柱形垫均表现出电压可控的粘附力调制。3 kV下平面垫约为0.11–0.13 N；圆柱形垫在5 N预载下达到约0.16 N。

Conclusion: 结果表明可打印出内建电控粘附与摩擦功能的部件，突破了传统平面静电粘附垫的几何限制，是多材料增材制造与静电粘附结合的一步探索。

Abstract: This paper investigates multi-material 3D printing as a method to fabricate non-planar structures with 3D-printed electrode patterns for electroadhesion. We printed flat electroadhesion pads as a planar benchmark and cylindrical pads as a non-planar demonstration, with conductive interdigitated electrodes 3D printed as part of the structure. Normal-force measurements showed voltage-controlled modulation in both geometries. At 3 kV, the flat pads generated approximately 0.11-0.13 N, while the cylindrical pads reached approximately 0.16 N at 5 N preload. These results demonstrate a step toward printed parts with built-in, electrically controlled adhesion and friction, beyond conventional planar electroadhesion pads.

</details>


### [172] [Calibrated Probabilistic Obstruction Reasoning with Vision-Language Models for Grasping in Clutter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18718)
*Thanh-Tuan Tran,Ngoc-Chien Chu,Thanh Nguyen Canh,Nak Young Chong,Nguyen-Viet Ha,Xiem HoangVan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CPOR-Grasp：标定式概率遮挡推理框架，将VLM/深度/amodal掩码证据标定融合为遮挡概率，归纳有效遮挡图分布并边缘化，支持认证决策与主动延迟，在UNOBench上显著优于SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从杂乱场景中取目标需判断直接抓取、移除阻挡物还是延迟决策。现有方法固定单一遮挡图或移除策略，忽视场景解释的不确定性；依赖未标定的VLM预测；成对遮挡关系可能联合不一致；近似推理对丢弃假设的影响无保证。

Method: 标定并融合VLM、深度与amodal掩码线索估计遮挡概率；诱导有效遮挡图上的概率分布；对图分布边缘化计算目标可达性或某阻挡物应被移除的似然；仅保留最高概率图使推理可行，并推导丢弃概率质量的总变差界，从而实现认证决策、自适应停止与有原则的延迟。

Result: 在合成与真实UNOBench场景上超越SOTA基线：Gemini Robotics骨干上标定误差由0.1416降至0.0185；图截断在99.74%的决策上与精确推理一致，使用图数量减少56倍；真实实验平均成功率77.8%。

Conclusion: 将遮挡推理显式建模为概率图分布上的边缘化问题并提供近似误差界，可在杂乱抓取中获得可认证、可延迟的决策，兼顾精度与推理效率。

Abstract: Retrieving a target from clutter requires deciding whether to grasp the target, remove a blocker, or defer. Existing methods typically commit to a single obstruction graph or removal strategy, ignoring uncertainty across alternative scene interpretations. They also rely on miscalibrated vision-language model (VLM) predictions and can produce pairwise obstruction relations that are jointly inconsistent. Moreover, current approximations provide no guarantees about the impact of discarded hypotheses on the final decision. We propose CPOR-Grasp, a calibrated probabilistic obstruction-reasoning framework that propagates uncertainty from pairwise evidence to action decisions. CPOR-Grasp calibrates and fuses VLM, depth, and amodal-mask cues to estimate obstruction probabilities, induces a distribution over valid obstruction graphs, and marginalizes over these graphs to compute the likelihood that the target is accessible or that a given blocker should be removed. To make inference tractable, it retains only the highest-probability graphs and derives a total-variation bound on the discarded probability mass, enabling certified decisions, adaptive stopping, and principled deferral. On synthetic and real UNOBench scenes, CPOR-Grasp outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Calibration error decreases from 0.1416 to 0.0185 on the Gemini Robotics backbone, while graph truncation matches exact inference on 99.74\% of decisions using 56 times fewer graphs. In real-world experiments, CPOR-Grasp achieves a 77.8\% average success rate, surpassing SOTA baselines.

</details>


### [173] [PASSAGE: Scaling Scene-Aligned Motion Learning for Perceptive Humanoid Traversal in Cluttered Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18732)
*Yuxuan Ma,Zicheng Zeng,Chunlin Peng,Zhoujian Li,Zetong Zhao,Zhikai Zhang,Yunrui Lian,Han Xue,Sikai Liang,Weiyi Zhu,Mulin Chen,Chenghuai Lin,Jiayu Zeng,Yanwei An,Songan Zhang,Jiayuan Gu,Jilong Wang,Jingbo Wang,He Wang,Li Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PASSAGE 提出感知条件化的「规划器-追踪器」框架：用 100 小时 VR/惯性动捕的场景对齐人体运动数据训练条件流匹配规划器，配合感知型全身追踪器，实现人形机器人对未知杂乱地形的自主通行与行为组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人具备跨越、侧身挤过、低头钻过等运动能力，但从机载感知中自主选择并协调这些行为仍很困难。现有方法依赖任务特定的强化学习目标或人工整理的运动库，行为覆盖成本高、泛化差。作者希望用大规模场景对齐的人类运动数据替代技能标注与障碍专用策略。

Method: 数据：VR + 惯性动捕采集 100 小时、跨 1500 个杂乱场景的场景对齐人体运动。规划器：条件流匹配（flow matching）生成短时域参考轨迹，条件为运动历史、局部目标点与机器人中心多层高程图。追踪器：感知型全身追踪器以 50 Hz 执行并引入几何反馈；实时分块（real-time chunking）保证块间一致性；在冻结追踪器的前提下对规划器做 RL 后训练以提升闭环性能。无需技能标注或障碍专用策略。

Result: 仿真中通过组件消融量化各阶段贡献；三个独立训练种子下，数据规模从 6 h 扩到 100 h 使留出场景平均无碰撞成功率由 48.1% 提升到 68.9%，加入场景增强的最终模型达 70.3%。全机载系统集成自中心 3D LiDAR 感知、在线占据建图、6.25 Hz 规划与 50 Hz 控制，运行于 Jetson AGX Orin；在 50 个未见实物布局上完成无需预建地图与离板计算的通行测试。

Conclusion: 单一「规划器-追踪器」组合可在无技能标注条件下选择并组合通行行为，泛化到未见几何；性能随动捕数据规模呈明显扩展趋势，且系统可完全机载实时运行并完成真机通行验证。工作展示了以场景对齐人体动捕数据 + 流匹配规划 + 感知全身控制实现人形复杂地形通行的可行路径。

Abstract: Humanoid robots can step over, squeeze past, and duck under obstacles, but learning to select and coordinate these behaviors from onboard perception remains challenging. Many existing approaches rely on task-specific reinforcement-learning objectives or curated motion libraries, making broad behavioral coverage costly. We present PASSAGE, a perception-conditioned planner--tracker framework for humanoid traversal. Using virtual reality and inertial motion capture, we collect 100 h of scene-aligned human motion across 1,500 cluttered scenes. A conditional flow-matching planner generates short-horizon references from motion history, a local destination, and a robot-centric multi-layer elevation map, while a perceptive whole-body tracker executes them at 50 Hz with geometric feedback. Real-time chunking promotes inter-chunk consistency, and planner-side RL post-training under the frozen tracker further improves closed-loop performance. Without skill annotations or obstacle-specific policies, one planner--tracker pair selects and composes traversal behaviors across unseen geometries. In simulation, component ablations quantify the contribution of each stage. Across three independent training seeds, scaling captured data from 6 to 100 h increases mean contact-free success from 48.1% to 68.9% on held-out scenes, while the final model with validated scene augmentation reaches 70.3%. The fully onboard system integrates egocentric 3D LiDAR perception, online occupancy mapping, 6.25 Hz planning, and 50 Hz control on a Jetson AGX Orin; tests across 50 unseen physical layouts demonstrate traversal without prebuilt maps or offboard computation.

</details>


### [174] [Active perception for robotic harvesting: 3D reconstruction and localisation of tomatoes hidden within clusters in a Mediterranean greenhouse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18738)
*Fernando Cañadas-Aránega,Rowan Border,José C. Moreno,José L. Blanco-Claraco*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 面向地中海温室番茄簇采摘的遮挡果实3D重建与定位流水线，结合NBV主动视角规划、SOR去噪、区域生长分割、DBSCAN遮挡果实恢复与3D位姿估计，仿真验证下精度>90%、平均召回82.8%、mIoU 80.7%、质心RMSE 4.2mm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有采摘视觉方案多针对孤立生长果实（苹果、甜椒、桃），固定传感器无法感知簇生蔬菜中被遮挡的果实；温室场景植物几何复杂、遮挡严重，需要能主动获取视角并恢复隐藏果实几何的完整感知流水线。

Method: 五阶段流水线：i) AgriSEE Next Best View主动规划采集点云；ii) 统计离群点移除(SOR)随机噪声滤波；iii) 区域生长(RG)表面分类与分割；iv) DBSCAN聚类隔离与恢复遮挡果实；v) 3D位姿（位置+朝向）估计；提取完整簇几何以识别部分隐藏番茄。

Result: 在多个不同遮挡等级的仿真场景中评估，仿真框架对真实条件做了严格校验；精度>90%，平均召回82.8%，mIoU 80.7%，质心估计RMSE仅4.2mm，重复性好。
